**Author / Provenance:** Antigravity + Principal Morphism Architecture Research Subagent (`pro`)  
**Date:** 2026-11-22  
**Target Ecosystem:** Webizen AI Synth (`0.0.41.15`, `C:\github\synth`), Hypermedia-Production (`C:\github\hypermedia-production`), QualiaDB (`C:\github\qualiaDB`), Etalon (`C:\github\etalon`), and Ecopunk (`C:\github\ecopunk`)

## Executive Summary

While `hypermedia-production` (`crates/asset-production-vibe`) and `synth` (`synth_produce_present`) currently register and dispatch **150 category-theoretic morphisms** (`OPERATIONS.len() == ontology::MORPHISMS.len() == 150`), a source-level audit against:
1. the **8,997 functions across 69 modules** in `hypermedia-production/audit-docs/FUNCTIONS.md`,
2. the **9 core subsystems + `.10d` v2 container codecs** in `qualiaDB` (`domains`, `modalities`, `solvers`, `specialized_libs`, `wgsl_forge`, `qualia-vision`, `qualia-audio`, `webizen-render`, `semantic_instruments`, `container_10d`),
3. the **8 `VibeNamespaceProvider` modules and GPU/simulation engines** in `etalon` (`etalon-render`, `etalon-sim`, `etalon-signal`, `etalon-reconstruct`, `etalon-wgsl-forge`, `etalon-vibe`), and
4. the **7-Layer Multi-Scale Computational Hierarchy (`L0`–`L6`)** and **Ecopunk 120-packet production backlog** (`ECO-001`–`ECO-120`),

reveals that **the perceived capability gap is not primarily a lack of underlying Rust algorithms, but a severe exposure, handle-typing, and wiring bottleneck inside `asset-production-vibe`**.

Specifically, this study classifies the gaps into **four actionable categories**:
- **Architectural & Handle Bottlenecks (Section 1.2):** `asset-production-vibe` restricts workspace handles (`Artifact`, `lib.rs:103-111`) to 7 primitive variants (`Profile`, `Mesh`, `Image`, `Rgba`, `Rgba16`, mono `Audio`, and opaque `Encoded`), lacking composable handles for attributed meshes, two-tier `.10d` scene graphs (`AREF`/`IOVR`/`INST`), `SKL2` skeletal rigs, NLA/animation clips, 3D/10D fields (`FieldLattice`), BSDF/MaterialX graphs, or multi-channel/Ambisonic audio. Furthermore, all 150 `vibescript-abi` descriptors collapse parameter schemas into a single untyped byte-slice (`DEFAULT_PARAMS`, `ontology/mod.rs:156`).
- **Category A — Implemented Kernels Missing Morphism Exposure (Section 2):** Hundreds of production-ready solvers already exist in `hypermedia-production`, `qualiaDB`, `etalon`, and `synth` (MCP/CLI), but cannot be invoked or composed inside `.vibe` scripts via `synth_produce_present`.
- **Category B — Partially Wired, Lossy, or Fixture-Stubbed Morphisms in the Current 150 (Section 3):**
  - 13 geometry morphisms (`mesh_edit_policy.rs:4-13`) strip UVs, normals, tangents, materials, and skin weights unless `attribute_policy="discard"` is passed.
  - `compile_10d` and `compile_10d_pbr` seal hardcoded dummy 10-float `HOLO` (`0x0306`) records and swallow v2 upgrade errors rather than fitting real harmonic bands or Hamiltonian trajectories.
  - ~13 Wave-13 domain-extension morphisms (`120..=143`) execute hardcoded internal smoke-test fixtures (e.g., 1-wall IFC4, 2×2 Cholesky + 4×4×4 LBM, 1-triangle cloth, 4-point Kriging, mock `AccessibleDocSnapshot`, 3-cell `=SUM(A1:B1)` spreadsheet) instead of operating on caller data.
- **Category C — True Missing Kernels, Unified 250-Morphism Proposed Catalog Across `L0`–`L6`, Domain Deep Dives, Steganographic Provenance, `rust-audit` AST Exposure Verification, Unified Report 1 + Report 2 Frontier Category-Theoretic, `L6` Multi-Scale Closure & Bidirectionality Suite (`#181`–`#240`), Specialist Semantic Metrology & Accessibility Suite (`#241`–`#250`), and Codex Swarm ### 1.2 Architectural Bottlenecks in `asset-production-vibe`
1. **Narrow 7-Variant `Artifact` Enum (`crates/asset-production-vibe/src/lib.rs:103-111`):**
   ```rust
   pub enum Artifact {
       Profile(Vec<[f64; 2]>),
       Mesh(Mesh),                         // bare positions + triangles + AABB only!
       Image(Raster),                      // opaque RGB8
       Rgba(graphics::Rgba),               // straight-alpha RGBA8
       Rgba16(radiance_precision::Rgba16), // straight-alpha RGBA16
       Audio { rate: u32, samples: Vec<f32> }, // mono f32 only!
       Encoded(Resource),                  // serialized bytes (only variant exported to files)
   }
   ```
   Because `qualia_core_db::render::assets::Mesh` holds only `positions: Vec<[f32; 3]>`, `triangles: Vec<[u32; 3]>`, `min`, and `max`, **every intermediate mesh handle in `AssetWorkspace` is structurally incapable of carrying UVs, normals, tangents, vertex colors, material slots, skin weights, or morph targets**.
2. **Untyped ABI Descriptors (`crates/asset-production-vibe/src/ontology/mod.rs:156-185`):**
   Every `AgenticMorphismSpec` uses the macro `morphism_func!` with `DEFAULT_PARAMS: &[ParamSpec::slice("args_record", PrimitiveKind::U8, 0, u32::MAX)]`, `EffectSet::PURE_COMPUTE`, and `ReversibilityKind::PureNoState`. The VibeScript compiler and MCP clients cannot statically validate handle types, physical QUDT units, or argument schemas before runtime execution.
3. **Split Execution Hosts (`synth_produce_present` vs `vibe_invoke`):**
   `synth_produce_present` runs `asset_production_vibe::AssetHost` (which knows the 150 hypermedia morphisms plus `qualia.anatomy.pack_reference_body` when compiled with `--features atlas`, `synth/src/production/source.rs:223`), but **rejects** `graph_query`, `graph_stage`, `aura_validate`, and all ~220+ STEM/ML/Chemistry/WGSL-Forge/Governance operations registered in `qualia_core_db::poet_host::invoke` (`vibe_invoke`) and `etalon_vibe::ETALON_VIBE_PROVIDERS`.

   - **Quantum Biology & QPU (`quantum_biology/`, `qpu_bridge/`, `qualia-q-forge`):** Radical-pair magnetoreception / photosynthetic exciton coherence, QGroup VQE scheduler, and `#![no_std]` OpenQASM statevector simulator.
   - **Multi-Scale Homogenization & Cartoon Physics (`physics_simulation/`):** `homogenization_{mech,acoust,hydro,optic}.rs` (upscaling `L3`/`L2` microstructure RVE to `L0` continuum tensors) and `cartoon_deformation.rs` (4-regime squash-and-stretch with singular-value wafer floor $\tilde{\lambda} = \max(\lambda, \varepsilon_{\text{wafer}})$ and apex hang-time).
   - **Computational Geometry (`computational_geometry/`):** `attribute_transfer` (preserving UVs/corners/skins/FEM across collapses), `botanical_hulls`, `branch_graph_surface`, `canopy_radiation` (`soc_sky_dome_16`, `solar_track_sources`, `integrate_canopy_radiation`), `convex_decomposition`, `corefine_3d`, `curve_skeleton`, `ddg_operators`, `differential_growth`, `dual_quaternion_skinning`, `heat_geodesics`, `medial_axis_3d`, `nurbs_fitting`, `pbd_softbody`, `poisson_disk`, `screened_poisson`, `space_colonization`, `tet_quality_improve`, `tree_skeleton_3d`, `turtle_3d`, `vr_filtration` (persistent homology).
6. **`wgsl_forge` (`qualia-core-db/src/wgsl_forge/*`):**
   - Multi-target shader compiler (`wgsl`, `spirv`, `msl`, `hlsl`, `ptx`, `cuda_c`, `coopmat`, `df64`), Naga validator, Roofline performance model, schedule autotuner, CPU/GPU parity oracle, and compute DAG executor.
7. **`qualia-vision` (`crates/qualia-vision`):**
   - YuNet face detection, SFace recognition, MediaPipe 468-landmark mesh, CHROM/POS rPPG cardiac & respiration sensing, Eulerian color & motion magnification (`eulerian_color_magnify`, `eulerian_motion_magnify`), liveness PAD, Macenko histopathology stain deconvolution, Voronoi-Otsu nuclear segmentation, Crocker-Grier particle tracking, super-resolution (`sr/`), and fiduciary consent/policy gates.
8. **`qualia-audio` (`crates/qualia-audio`):**
   - Multipitch (`melodia`, `klapuri`), beat/onset tracking, chord/key/tuning detection, CQT + Griffin-Lim inversion, **SF2 SoundFont / SFZ / DLS polyphonic synth**, MIDI 2.0 UMP + Scala `.scl`/`.kbm` microtuning, FDN Hadamard reverb, Kaiser-sinc resampling, **DNSMOS / NISQA / PESQ-subset** audio quality assessment, learned 2-stem source separation, and FSQ/RVQ neural audio tokens.
9. **`webizen-render` & `semantic_instruments`:**
   - `VolumetricRenderer`, MVT/PMTiles curved labels, atmospheric limb, `RdfHolographScene`, `TemporalFrameScheduler`; and 11 native `semantic_instruments` evaluators (`evaluate_contribution_lifecycle`, `evaluate_wellfair_fairness_rights_review`, `evaluate_licence_transition_readiness`, W3C Verifiable Credential & OpenBadge issuers).

| Sub-Class | Affected Morphism IDs & Source Locations | Concrete Defect / Bottleneck |

Destructive Geometry Edits** | 13 morphisms in `crates/asset-production-vibe/src/mesh_edit_policy.rs:4-13` (`boolean_3d`, `simplify`, `radial_euler`, `bevel`, `inset`, `solidify`, `bisect_plane`, `subdivide_catmull_clark`, `evaluate_field`, `realize_instances`, `mesh_to_sdf_brick`, `sweep_assembly_profile`, `unwrap_slim`) | `prepare_geometry_edit` strips all UVs, normals, tangents, materials, submeshes, vertex colors, and textures and **fails closed** unless `attribute_policy="discard"` is passed — bypassing `qualia_core_db::specialized_libs::computational_geometry::attribute_transfer`. |
| **B2. Dummy `HOLO` v2 & Missing `AREF`/`INST`** | `hypermedia.manifold.compile_10d` (`geometry.rs:324-326`), `compile_10d_pbr` (`manifold_ops.rs:1228-1234`), `sweep_assembly_profile` (`manifold_assembly_bim_erosion.rs:344-361`) | Pass a static dummy 10-float `HOLO` array `[0.0, 3.0, 2.0, centroid[0..3], 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.5]` and ignore `upgrade_legacy_10d_to_holographic_v2` errors via `.unwrap_or(sealed)`. None encode `MSTR` (`L0`–`L6`), `CBLD`, or two-tier `AREF`/`IOVR`/`INST` scene references. |

BSDF & GLB Import Rejections** | `hypermedia.manifold.compile_10d_pbr` (`manifold_ops.rs:895-900`), `compile_bsdf_graph` (`bsdf_procedural.rs:77,280-297`), `import_mesh` (`manifold_ops.rs:577-584,1281-1308`) | `compile_bsdf_graph` defaults `coat_weight=0.2` and emits `"energy_conserving": null`; `compile_10d_pbr` rejects any BSDF graph where `coat_weight != 0.0` or closure != `"principled_v2"`. `import_mesh` rejects OBJ `vt`/`vn`/`mtllib` and GLB `skins`, `animations`, morph `targets`, `COLOR_0`, and KHR material extensions despite `qualia-core-db` supporting them. |

Dropped Instance Rotations & Bare Botany/Anatomy** | `instance_on_points` & `realize_instances` (`fields_instances.rs:431,508,553`), `fspm_vascular_grow` (`sdf_botany.rs:278-524`), `compile_vertebrate_anatomy_10d` (`botanical_vertebrate_10d.rs:152,299-326,484`) | `instance_on_points` accepts `"rotations"` in `fields()` but never reads it. `fspm_vascular_grow` outputs bare 12-sector cylinders (no leaves/roots/10D). `compile_vertebrate_anatomy_10d` runs layer peel on dummy `[0,0.5,0]` vectors and discards the output; `emit_emf_qac=true` hardcodes wallaby/ghost-gum rigs regardless of `species_id`. |

Motion blur takes 1 global scalar 2D vector. SDFGI shades a 2D image assuming flat normal `[0,1,0]` and max 4 Y-probes. `Rgba16` is rejected by 26 of 30 radiance ops and refuses `PQ`/`HLG`. |

## 4. Category C — Prerequisite Handle Extensions & Proposed New Morphisms Across `L0`–`L6`

### 4.1 Prerequisite Workspace Handle & `vibescript-abi` Upgrades (`crates/asset-production-vibe/src/lib.rs:103-111` & `qualiaDB/crates/vibescript-abi/src/descriptor.rs`)
To eliminate attribute stripping, flattening, and signature mismatches across all 137 proposed morphisms, `AssetWorkspace` (`crates/asset-production-vibe/src/lib.rs`) and `vibescript-abi` should standardize **16 typed Arc-shared Copy-on-Write (CoW) handle variants** plus per-morphism ABI effect/determinism/unit metadata:
1. `AttributedMesh(Arc<AttributedMesh>)` — preserves `UV0`/`UV1`, normals, MikkT tangents, `COLOR_0`, submesh material slots, `SKIN_JOINTS/WEIGHTS`, `MRP1` morph deltas, and protected datum/articular face masks across `manifold.*`, `surface.*`, and `fabrication.*` edits.
2. `SceneGraph10d(Arc<SceneGraph10d>)` — two-tier PGA $\text{SE}(3)$ hierarchy holding `AREF` leaf asset URIs/SHA-256 digests, `IOVR` per-instance overrides, `INST` motor tables, lights, cameras, and semantic `NQuin` bindings (`Cat A`: backed by `qualia-core-db::container_10d::archetype_ref_section`).
3. `BranchGraph10d(Arc<BranchGraph10d>)` — topological DAG/anastomosing network carrying per-edge radius, Pipe/Murray $\alpha$, and physiological state $(\Psi/P_{\text{turgor}}, K_{\text{hyd}}, C_{\text{metab}}, I_{\text{def}})$ (`Cat A`: backed by `qualia-core-db::computational_geometry::branch_graph_surface`).
4. `SkeletalRig(Arc<Skl2Rig>)` — second-generation 64-byte `SKL2` joints, `SwingTwistConeLimits`, loop-closure constraints, musculotendon pathways, 5 solver regimes, and `SSM1` basis (`Cat A`: backed by `container_10d::skeletal_section::skl2`).
5. `AnimationClip(Arc<AnimClip>)` — multi-track PGA motor curves, `MRP1` curves, ABSM blend-space states, NLA `ActionChannelbag` strips, and root-motion trajectories.
6. `FieldLattice(Arc<FieldLattice>)` — unified sparse/dense 2D/3D/10D grid handle unifying `SDFB` (`0x0307`), O-Voxel `VOX0`, `OCC1`, `.m64` strata/fluid grids, `.emf` `Q42EMF64` field volumes, and clinical LPS tomography volumes.
7. `MaterialGraph(Arc<BsdfShaderIr>)` — energy-conserving OpenPBR / MaterialX 1.39 IR + spectral/NPR closures + camera-log/ICC/LUT color pipeline.
8. `RadianceLattice(Arc<RadianceLattice>)` — unified `u8`/`u16`/`f16`/`f32` raster lattice with explicit primaries and transfer tag (`Linear`, `sRGB`, `PQ_ST2084`, `HLG`, `ACEScct`, `LogC4`).
9. `AovBundle(Arc<AovBundle>)` — multi-plane deep/planar `RadianceLattice` bundle keyed by AOV semantic IRI (`Beauty`, `Albedo`, `Normal`, `Depth`, `Velocity`, `CryptomatteId`) (`Cat C`: new multi-channel handle).
10. `CurveGroomBundle(Arc<CurveGroomBundle>)` — UV-rooted Catmull-Rom / Cosserat strand guides for hair, fur, feathers, surgical sutures, and 3D wire harnesses (`Cat B/C`: wraps `etalon-render::skeletal::cosserat_rod`).
11. `ParticleSet(Arc<ParticleSet>)` — typed 3D point/particle cloud for ballistic water spray, granular MPM grains, and noisy LiDAR/sensor twins (`Cat C`: new handle & `.10d` `0x0308` field kind).
12. `AudioMultiChannel(Arc<AudioBuffer>)` — unifies Mono, Stereo, 5.1/7.1/7.1.4 Surround, Holosonic array, and Ambisonics ACN/SN3D (orders 1..5).
13. `HmcBundle(Arc<HmcWorkspaceView>)` — multi-modal `.hmc` (`QBDL`) assembly staging `.q42`, `.10d`, `.m64`, `.emf`, `.qac`, `.p64`, and `.vibe` resources prior to merit-order HTTP-Range sealing (`Cat A`: backed by `qualia-core-db::bundle::{format, selective_streamer}`).
14. `ComputeCartridge { kind: P64 | Np64, bytes: Arc<[u8]> }` — typed handle for `.p64` neural/statistical surrogate weights (`Cat A`: `q42::p64_weight` & `modalities::p64_cartridge::P64EXT01`) and `Anvil` `.np64` silicon capability profiles (`Cat C`: new `q42::np64_hardware`).
15. `TabularCube(Arc<TabularFrame>)` — columnar/multi-sheet workbook handle bridging `chronicle-tabular-engine`, GeoParquet, and `.q42` observation cubes (`Cat A/B`).
16. `StegoPayloadPacket(Arc<StegoBitPacket>)` — Reed-Solomon / LDPC error-corrected, sync-framed creation-tree steganographic bitstream handle (`Cat C`: new handle for `#134`–`#137`).
* **Ground-Truth `vibescript-abi` Descriptor Fix (`ontology/mod.rs:156-184, 316-318` & `vibescript-abi/src/descriptor.rs:232-298`):** `qualiaDB/crates/vibescript-abi/src/effects.rs:7-100` **already defines** `EffectSet` (`PURE_COMPUTE`, `GRAPH_READ`, `GRAPH_MUTATE`, `HARDWARE_IO`, `EXTERNAL_NET`, `CRYPTO_VAULT`, `TASK_SPAWN`), `DeterminismGuarantee` (`DeterministicExact`, `DeterministicTolerance`, `NonDeterministicExternal`), and `ReversibilityKind` (`PureNoState`, `StagedReversible`, `CompensatingAction`, `IrreversibleExternal`). The local defect is that `asset-production-vibe/src/ontology/mod.rs` hardcodes `PURE_COMPUTE` / `DeterministicExact` / `PureNoState` (`"Irreversible"` in `morphism_to_json`) across all 150 morphisms, and `ParamSpec` (`descriptor.rs:232-241`) lacks QUDT physical unit IRI and numeric domain-bound fields. (Note: `vibescript-abi` exists in both `qualiaDB` and `vibe-script`; `WP-00` must keep both synchronized.)

## 5. Deep Dive: Why Water (Waves, Breaking Waves, Reflections, Refraction, Caustics) & Natural-Phenomena Assets Currently Fall Short

A end-to-end trace across `hypermedia-production`, `qualiaDB` (`PortalGpu` / `WaterGpu`), and `etalon` (`etalon-render` / `etalon-sim`) confirms why current water and environmental assets appear as flat cyan/blue sheets without realistic wave dispersion, overturning breakers, scene reflections, depth refraction, or caustics.

### 5.1 What the Current 150-Morphism Catalog Actually Exposes for Water & Fluids
1. **Zero dedicated water/wave simulation morphisms exist in `kinetics_sim`:** `crates/asset-production-vibe/src/kinetics_sim/mod.rs:11-40` contains only 6 rigid/navigation/NLA morphisms (`interpolate_motor_fti`, `step_island_xpbd`, `query_direct_space`, `navigate_funnel_orca`, `evaluate_nla_channelbag`, `step_simulation_zone`).
2. **`geodesy.advect_m64_grid` (`m64_continuum.rs:71-391`) ignores caller grids:** Runs a 2D Jacobi Poisson projection + MacCormack advection + Eikonal redistancing on a **hardcoded internal sinusoidal vortex + wind field** (`lines 105-121`), capped at `grid_side <= 128` (`line 83`), emitting `.m64` bytes and a 2D RGBA8 heatmap.
3. **`geodesy.couple_continuum_xpbd` (`m64_continuum.rs:395-500`) is a 1-line scalar formula:** Computes scalar Bekker-Wong soil sinkage and a single scalar Archimedes buoyancy number (`lines 493-496`), emitting no wave mesh, heightfield, or velocity field.
4. **`kinetics.solve_continuum_fem_lbm` (`kinetics_fem_garment_blocks.rs:9-109`) runs on a hardcoded $4\times 4\times 4$ toy lattice:** Calls `LbmGrid3D::new(4, 4, 4, 0.02)` (`line 36`) and ignores the input mesh geometry beyond counting its vertices and triangles (`lines 11-18, 104-107`).
5. **`surface.compile_bsdf_graph` (`bsdf_procedural.rs:217-230, 280-296`) is a 2D sphere preview:** Accepts `closure="fournier_forand_water"`, but renders only an analytic CPU sphere swatch and explicitly records `"fournier_forand_water" => "analytic sphere preview; no participating medium"` and `"energy_conservation_status": "not measured"`.

### 5.2 Four Root Deficits in Live Water & Ocean Rendering (`WaterGpu` + `water.wgsl` + `etalon-render`)

```
Current Live Water Path (WaterGpu -> water.wgsl):
  Vertex:   height = sin(x) * cos(z) * amp * shore_fade  (vertical Y only; 0 horizontal trochoidal pinch)
  BindGrp:  @group(0..2) only (NO sky/IBL cubemap, NO planar/SSR texture, NO scene_depth / scene_color)
  Fragment: reflected_tint = mix(const_cyan_grey, const_sky_blue, clamp(reflected_ray.y, 0, 1))
            shore_scale    = 2D distance to mesh open edge (NOT vertical bathymetry y_water - y_terrain)
            caustics       = NONE (0 implementations)

Orphaned / Unwired Kernels Sitting in etalon-render & qualia-core-db:
  - SWE_SOLVER_WGSL (etalon-render/src/shaders/swe_solver.wgsl:1-259)        -> Never compiled or dispatched
  - HYDROLOGIC_OPTICS_WGSL (shaders/hydrologic_optics.wgsl:1-102)            -> Never compiled or called
  - IblCubemapGenerator (etalon-render/src/ibl_cubemap.rs:1-611)             -> Never bound to WaterGpu
  - evaluate_water_wave_and_foam (geo_viewport/sky_limb.rs:168-238)          -> CPU-only, unused by WaterGpu
  - fluid_dynamics.wgsl (qualia-core-db/src/shaders/fluid_dynamics.wgsl:1-94)-> Unwired 1D-indexed buffer
```

1. **Wave Kinematics & Missing Ocean Spectra (`water.wgsl:36-68`, `gerstner.rs:144-190`):**
   - **Live GPU Shader (`etalon-render/src/shaders/water.wgsl:50-59`):** Evaluates a single separable standing wave product (`height = sin_x * cos_z * water.wave.y * shore_fade`) displaced **strictly vertically in $Y$** (`local = position + vec3<f32>(0.0, height, 0.0)`). There is zero horizontal trochoidal $(X,Z)$ crest sharpening, zero multi-band dispersion ($\omega^2 = g k \tanh(kd)$), zero directional spreading, and zero capillary ripples.
   - **Etalon CPU `SimdGerstnerSpectrum` bug (`etalon-render/src/water_surface/gerstner.rs:33, 174`):** Declares `MAX_GERSTNER_HARMONICS = 8` and `steepness: f32`, yet `sample_surface` (`line 174`) only accumulates `height += w.amplitude * sin_theta;` (purely vertical displacement, omitting horizontal trochoidal displacement and the Jacobian crest-folding determinant).
   - **Unwired CPU Trochoidal Helper (`etalon-render/src/geo_viewport/sky_limb.rs:168-238`):** `evaluate_water_wave_and_foam` *does* implement 4-octave horizontal+vertical trochoidal Gerstner displacement (`q * amp * di * cos_p`) and finite-depth Airy dispersion (`tanh(k * water_depth_m)`), but is CPU-only and never wired into `water.wgsl`, `WaterGpu`, or `asset-production-vibe`.
   - **Missing Everywhere:** JONSWAP, Pierson-Moskowitz, and TMA directional wave spectra, 2D inverse-FFT (Tessendorf) height/slope/Jacobian tiles (`jonswap` = 0 hits, `tessendorf` = 0 hits across all workspaces), and Kelvin wake synthesis.

2. **3D Breaking Waves & Overturning Free Surfaces (`swe_solver.wgsl:1-259`):**
   - **Orphaned 2.5D Shallow-Water Solver:** `etalon-render/src/shaders/swe_solver.wgsl:1-259` implements a well-balanced Kurganov-Petrova central-upwind 2.5D Saint-Venant Shallow Water Equation (SWE) compute shader (`rgba32float`: $h, hu, hv, \text{tracer}$) with Manning bed friction and wet/dry threshold $10^{-4}$. However, across all 622 files in `qualia-core-db/src/render`, `etalon-render/src`, and `asset-production-vibe/src`, `SWE_SOLVER_WGSL` is only declared as a string constant (`shaders/mod.rs:44`) — it is **never compiled into a `ComputePipeline`, never dispatched in `PortalGpu`, and never sampled by `water.wgsl`**.
   - **Why 2.5D SWE Cannot Break in 3D:** Even once wired, a 2.5D SWE represents water as a single-valued heightfield $h(x,z)$ and **mathematically cannot overturn in $Y$** to form plunging breaker tubes, curling wave lips, or air-entraining splash sheets.
   - **Missing Everywhere:** There is **zero** 3D FLIP/APIC solver, **zero** Level-Set / Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) two-phase air-water free-surface solver, **zero** 3D SPH solver with surface tension and vorticity confinement, and **zero** 3D MPM free-surface solver (`qualia-core-db/src/specialized_libs/physics_simulation/cfd.rs:17-81` is only a 1D viscous Burgers solver $u_t + u u_x = \nu u_{xx}$).

3. **Reflections, Refraction, Bathymetry & Caustics (`water.rs:129-185`, `water.wgsl:77-110`):**
   - **Why Water Reflections Are Missing:** `WaterGpu` (`qualia-core-db/src/render/gpu/water.rs:149-184`) creates a pipeline layout binding only `mesh_frame_layout`, `mesh_model_layout`, and `WaterUniform`. It binds **no sky/environment cubemap, no planar reflection texture, no screen-space reflection (SSR) color/depth buffer, and no `EnvironmentLighting` uniform**. Consequently, `water.wgsl:106-110` fakes reflections by blending **two hardcoded RGB constants** by `reflected_ray.y`:
     ```wgsl
     let reflected_tint = mix(vec3<f32>(0.68, 0.77, 0.78), vec3<f32>(0.30, 0.53, 0.76), clamp(reflected_ray.y, 0.0, 1.0));
     ```
     Surrounding trees, terrain, clouds, and structures are completely invisible in water reflections. Meanwhile, `etalon-render/src/ibl_cubemap.rs:1-611` (`IblCubemapGenerator`, `IBL_CUBEMAP_CONVOLVE_WGSL`) already implements 6-face procedural sky cubemap generation + 5-mip GGX specular prefiltering + split-sum BRDF LUT, **yet `PortalGpu` never instantiates or binds it**.
   - **Why Bathymetry & Refraction Fail:** `water.wgsl:96-105` explicitly comments: *"Mesh boundary distance is a shoreline attribute, not measured bathymetry."* `WaterGpu::replace_geometry_with_budget` (`water.rs:340-385`) computes 2D distance to open mesh boundary edges (`shoreline.rs`), **not vertical water-column depth** $y_{\text{water}} - y_{\text{terrain}}$. Furthermore, `water.wgsl` never binds `scene_depth` or `scene_color`, performs zero Snell refraction (`refract(-view_dir, normal, 1.0 / 1.333)`), and simply blends constant shallow (`[0.34, 0.47, 0.33]`) and deep RGB values.
   - **Orphaned Hydrologic Optics (`hydrologic_optics.wgsl:1-102`, `optics.rs:18-286`):** `etalon-render` already implements Cauchy chromatic IOR dispersion, Henyey-Greenstein particulate Mie scattering ($g \approx 0.85\text{--}0.92$), Beer-Lambert spectral transmittance, and turbid backscatter veils (for clear streams, tannin blackwater, and clay turbidity), **but `HYDROLOGIC_OPTICS_WGSL` is orphaned** (never compiled into any shader module or called by `water.wgsl`).
   - **Refractive Caustics:** Completely absent across all repositories (`caustic` = 0 code implementations; only 1 doc comment in `optics.rs:272`).

4. **Secondary Multi-Phase Phenomena (Whitecap Foam, Spray, Bubbles, Shoreline Wetmaps):**
   - Foam in `water.wgsl:98-101` is restricted to a narrow mesh-edge strip (`shore_width = clamp(input.shore_scale * 0.22, 0.0001, 0.008)`) modulated by a 1-octave sine ripple. There is zero open-water crest-folding whitecap foam (Jacobian determinant $J < J_{\text{thresh}}$), zero ballistic spray or bubble particle advection, and zero shoreline capillary darkening (`wetmap`) on adjacent rocks and sand.

### 5.3 Five Additional Natural-Phenomena & Physical Asset Deficit Domains

| Domain | Exists in Rust / WGSL (Unwired or Partial) | Exposed in Current 150 Morphisms | Genuinely Missing Across Repos |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Atmosphere, Sky, Volumetric Clouds & Weather** | • `etalon-render/src/shaders/sky.wgsl:74-109` (analytic gradient + sun disk)<br>• `etalon-render/src/geo_viewport/sky_limb.rs:9-156` (spherical Rayleigh/Mie)<br>• `etalon-sim/src/environment/atmosphere.rs:34-100` (`BarometricState`, Espy `LCL` condensation altitude, Tetens vapor pressure, ABL wind)<br>• `etalon-render/src/shaders/precipitation.wgsl:1-177` (depth-occluded rain/snow/sleet GPU compute shader — **orphaned**, never instantiated by `PortalGpu`)<br>• `terrain-erosion-solvers/src/{lacustrine_fill.rs,snow_accumulation.rs}` | • `radiance.solve_sdfgi_froxel` (`gpu_gi.rs:191-267`): 2D RGBA8 post-process using `sky_limb` + single-scatter HG fog<br>• `manifold.erode_geomorphic_heightfield` (`125`) omits both `fill_lakes` and `simulate_snow` | • **3D Volumetric Cloud Fields** (`volumetric_cloud=0`, `cumulus=0`): zero sparse 3D VDB/froxel cumulus/stratus/cumulonimbus density fields, Beer-Powder multiple scattering, or crepuscular god-rays<br>• Bruneton multi-scattering sky LUTs & ozone absorption in `sky.wgsl`<br>• Wiring `PRECIPITATION_WGSL` & snow accumulation into `PortalGpu` and `.10d` |

## 7. Deep Dive: Biology & Comprehensive Medical-Grade Anatomy Deficits

### 7.1 Systemic Diagnosis: Validators Without Generators & Ellipsoid Organ Proxies

Auditing `synth/src/model_validation.rs:122-650`, `synth/agent-instructions/species.md:1-38`, `qualia-core-db/src/render/{vertebrate_anatomy.rs, human_welfare_anatomy.rs}`, and `wellfare-core/src/anatomy/` exposes a fundamental three-way disconnect:

1. **`synth_validate_model` Enforces Clinical-Grade Topological Invariants That Zero Authoring Morphisms Construct:**
   - `verify_topological_and_radial_invariants` (`synth/src/model_validation.rs:126-650`) strictly checks:
     - **5 Radial Tissue Bands ($d \in [0, 1]$):** `"periosteum_capsule"` $[0.00, 0.08]$, `"ligament_deep_tendon"` $[0.05, 0.20]$, `"musculature"` $[0.15, 0.75]$, `"subcutis_fascia"` $[0.75, 0.92]$, and `"dermis_epidermis"` $[0.92, 1.00]$ (`lines 131-133`).
     - **Serous Cavity Containment:** Every `internal_organ` must declare `parent_cavity_id`, `suspended_by_id` (pericardium, pleura, mesentery, retroperitoneal fascia), and satisfy $\phi_{\text{cavity}}(\mathbf{x}) \le -\epsilon_{\text{clearance}}$ (`lines 133, 140-160`).
     - **Barycentric UV Entheses & Retinacular Pulleys:** Muscles/ligaments require $\ge 2$ `enthesis_bindings` with $\max \Delta UV \le 10^{-4}\text{ m}$ and `retinaculum_or_compartment_id` across polyaxial joints (`lines 145-163`).
     - **Soft-Tissue Incompressibility:** $\det(\mathbf{F}) \in [0.85, 1.15]$ (`line 166`).
     - **4 Dynamic Pathology Regimes:** Adipose hypertrophy/hyperplasia with `retinacula cutis` septa & visceral spillover (`lines 167-182`), benign `"displace_only"` encapsulated cysts (`lines 183-194`), `DOID`/`HP`/`MONDO`-gated invasive neoplasms (`lines 195-204`), and 0-mesh 2D Laplace-Beltrami cutaneous reaction-diffusion rashes (`lines 205-212`).
   - **Yet none of the 150 authoring morphisms construct serous cavity SDF clearances, `retinacula cutis` septa, retinacular pulleys, barycentric UV entheses (`FEMC` `VertexTetBinding`), or multi-compartment internal organ trees.**

2. **What `145 compile_vertebrate_anatomy_10d` Actually Emits (`vertebrate_anatomy.rs` & `human_welfare_anatomy.rs`):**
   - **Non-Human Comparative Profiles (`qualia-core-db/src/render/vertebrate_anatomy.rs:67-190`):** Emit **only 3 macro layers** (`Osteology`, `MyologyAndTendons`, `ExternalIntegument`; 17 submeshes total) with **zero internal organs**.
   - **Human Welfare Profiles (`qualia-core-db/src/render/human_welfare_anatomy.rs:344-1640`):** Emit an atlas summary claiming `"systems_covered": 17` (`botanical_vertebrate_10d.rs:301`). However, inspecting the geometry construction at `human_welfare_anatomy.rs:723-1006, 1448-1505` reveals that **every internal organ is a primitive geometric ellipsoid or cylinder shoved into `ZoologicalAnatomicalLayer::MyologyAndTendons` (`line 721`)**:
     - **Brain & Spinal Cord (`lines 725-759`):** 2 cerebral ellipsoids + 1 cylinder.
     - **Heart & Great Vessels (`lines 772-809`):** 1 ellipsoid + 2 cylinders.
     - **Lungs & Bronchi (`lines 822-833`):** 2 bare solid ellipsoids (`resp_lungs_bronchi`) with **zero bronchial tree or alveolar parenchyma**.
     - **Liver, Stomach, Entire Intestine, Kidneys, Spleen, Pancreas, Thyroid, Bladder, Uterus, Placenta, Fetus (`lines 847-997, 1448-1505`):** Each is a single `push_ellipsoid` call.

## 8. Deep Dive: Procedural Morphogenesis, Branching Systems, Homeostatic Flux & Two-Tier Terminal Amplification

Synthesizing the architectural principles from the **Procedural Morphisms in Hypermedia Generation** design study with a line-by-line audit of `qualia-core-db/src/specialized_libs/computational_geometry/`, `etalon-render/src/vegetation/`, and `asset-production-vibe/src/manifold_fields_sdf/sdf_botany.rs` establishes a unified **6-Pillar Morphogenesis Architecture** across flora, fauna, funga, and geomorphology:

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Pillar 1 & 3: Semantic Environment Fields (.10d FIEL / .m64) & Generative Graph Operator  │
│   Φ(x,t) = Σ w_k S_k(E_k(x,t)) - R(x,t)  (PAR light, soil moisture/NO3, tissue O2/VEGF)   │
│   Operators: Space Colonization (tree/loop) | L-System | CCO (dual-tree) | Laplacian/DLA │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                              ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Pillar 3, 4 & 5: Stateful Homeostatic Graph (.q42 DAG/Network + 4 Biophysical Bottlenecks)│
│   Per-Edge State: (Ψ / P_turgor,  K_hyd ∈ [0,1],  C_metab,  I_def)                        │
│   • Basipetal Pass (Tips -> Base): metabolic yield/demand Q_i + auxin apical dominance     │
│   • Acropetal Pass (Base -> Tips): proportional flux ΔF_i + self-pruning / deadwood       │
│   • Trauma & Biotic Perturbations: severance -> epicormic coppicing; K_hyd->0 -> VEGF     │
│     collateralogenesis; chronic stress σ -> reaction wood / Wolff bone; canker -> CODIT   │
│   • Acute vs Chronic: paraheliotropism/wilting vs Ψ_50 cavitation, marcescence vs drop    │
└──────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
                       ▼                                             ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Pillar 2: Regime A Macro-Scaffold Populator │ │ Pillar 6: Regime B Terminal Amplifier     │
│   • Bottom-up post-order Pipe/Murray scaling│ │   • Coarse .q42 tips carry compact Vogel  │
│     r_parent^α = Σ r_i^α  (α = 2.0..3.0)    │ │     / Bravais-Bravais descriptors:        │
│   • Min-work bifurcation angles cos(θ_1)    │ │     θ_k = k·φ, z_k = Σ L(j), r_k = R(z_k) │
│   • C^1 smooth crotch skinning via          │ │   • L(j)->0 telescopes shoots into umbels,│
│     branch_graph_surface + smooth_junction  │ │     panicles, spikes, capitula, acini     │
│   • Elliptical reaction wood & CODIT ribs   │ │   • Close LoD: Etalon WGSL compute inst   │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │   • Far LoD: Analytic SGGX cloud (0x0306) │
                                                └───────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 8.1 Pillar 1 — Decoupling the 4 Core Mathematical Branching Operators from Population
* **Current Code Reality:**
  - `144 hypermedia.manifold.fspm_vascular_grow` (`sdf_botany.rs:278-524`) is a hardcoded symmetric binary splitter ($r_{\text{child}} = 0.5^{1/3} r_{\text{parent}}$) emitting **disconnected 12-sector frustum cylinders** with zero environmental attractors, zero obstacle impedance, zero tropism, and zero anastomosis.
  - `qualia-core-db/.../computational_geometry/space_colonization.rs:135-292` (`space_colonization_3d`) and `etalon-render/src/vegetation/leaf_biophysics.rs:218` (2D 96-edge anastomosing leaf venation) exist in Rust (`Category A`) with **0 catalog morphism callers**. Meanwhile, 3D closed-loop anastomosing Space Colonization (`colonize_attractor_graph`, noted in a comment at `space_colonization.rs:10`) is unimplemented (`Category C`).
  - `computational_geometry/turtle_3d.rs:1-100` is an imperative `TurtleCmd` geometry interpreter, **not** a parametric/stochastic/context-sensitive L-system grammar rewriter (`0` implementations).
  - **Constrained Constructive Optimization (CCO)** and **Laplacian Growth / Diffusion-Limited Aggregation (DLA) / Physarum transport networks** have **0 implementations** across all repositories (`Category C`).
* **Morphism Remedy (`#64 hypermedia.manifold.colonize_branching_graph`):** Unifies all four mathematical models (**Space Colonization** with `anastomosisRate` loop fusion, **Parametric/Environmental L-Systems**, **Single & Dual-Tree CCO**, and **Laplacian/DLA/Physarum**) as pure topological skeleton operators over a bounding/tropism field $\Phi(\mathbf{x},t)$, outputting a `BranchGraph10d` handle completely decoupled from cross-sectional skinning.

### 8.2 Pillar 2 — Universal Cross-Sectional Allometric Scaling ($\alpha$) & $C^1$ Smooth Junction Skinning
* **Current Code Reality:**
  - `qualia-core-db/.../computational_geometry/branch_graph_surface/pipeline.rs:104-169` has **0 callers** in `hypermedia-production` and hardcodes `tapered_branch_field_into` with **`blend = 0.0`** (`lines 148-154`), explicitly warning: *"Uses hard minimum ... This can have C0 collar creases; smooth normals do not make the underlying surface C1."*
  - Immediately adjacent in `qualia-core-db/.../computational_geometry/smooth_junction/{mod.rs:1-100, project.rs:101-190}`, `smooth_junction::smooth_into` implements bounded gradient projection onto a positive smooth-union field (`blend in (0, 100]`) with adaptive edge refinement—yet it also has **0 callers** in `hypermedia-production`!
* **Morphism Remedy (`#65 hypermedia.manifold.populate_allometric_vascular_c1`):**
  - Performs a single **bottom-up post-order traversal** from terminal tips ($r_{\text{tip}}$) to the root using the generalized Pipe/Murray allometric power law:
    $$r_{\text{parent}}^\alpha = \sum_{i=1}^n r_i^\alpha$$
    covering **Da Vinci's area-preserving rule ($\alpha = 2.0$)** for sapwood pipes, **biomechanical wind-bending taper ($\alpha \approx 2.3\text{--}2.5$)** for large *Eucalyptus* limbs, and **Murray's Law ($\alpha = 3.0$)** for laminar arteries, veins, bronchi, and xylem micro-vessels, alongside minimum-work fluid bifurcation angles $\cos(\theta_1) = \frac{r_0^4 + r_1^4 - r_2^4}{2 r_0^2 r_1^2}$.
  - Composes `branch_graph_surface` directly into `smooth_junction::smooth_into` (and SDF `smin` volumetric `HOLO` records) so crotch junctions bulge organically with $C^1$ continuity, modulated by cross-sectional eccentricity (reaction wood) and wound-callus angular arcs.

### 8.3 Pillar 3 — Semantic Field Tropism $\Phi(\mathbf{x}, t)$, Stigmergy & Bidirectional Borchert-Honda / Hemodynamic Flux
* **Current Code Reality:**
  - `computational_geometry/canopy_radiation.rs:1-200` (Beer-Lambert PAR solar light extinction) has **0 callers** in `hypermedia-production`.
  - `etalon-render/src/vegetation/metamer_fspm.rs:101-300` caps at `MAX_METAMERS = 128` with a scalar carbon pool and has no two-pass basipetal/acropetal graph flux solver or stigmergic field write-back.
* **Morphism Remedy (`#66 hypermedia.kinetics.solve_homeostatic_graph_flux`):**
  - Evaluates local growth potential from `.10d` semantic fields and `.m64` matter impedance:
    $$\Phi(\mathbf{x}, t) = \sum_k w_k \cdot S_k(E_k(\mathbf{x}, t)) - R(\mathbf{x}, t)$$
    with **stigmergic write-back** (canopy nodes cast radiative occlusion cones into the PAR light lattice, producing emergent crown shyness and forest-edge leaning; roots write nutrient/moisture depletion zones into the rhizosphere; capillaries write $\text{O}_2$ perfusion into hypoxic tissue fields).
  - Executes two lightweight scalar passes per developmental step (**Borchert-Honda** in flora; **Poiseuille Wall Shear Stress remodeling** in fauna):
    1. **Basipetal Pass (Tips $\to$ Base):** Accumulates terminal metabolic yield/demand $Q_i$ and downward **auxin** apical-dominance inhibition.
    2. **Acropetal Pass (Base $\to$ Tips):** Splits flux proportionally to downstream conductivity and demand ($\Delta F_i = F_{\text{parent}} \frac{\sigma_i Q_i^\gamma}{\sum_j \sigma_j Q_j^\gamma}$), automatically starving shaded lower branches into `LivingSapwood -> SenescentDeadwood -> AbscisedStub`.

### 8.4 Pillar 4 — Injury, Trauma & Regenerative Morphisms as Graph Perturbations
Rather than authoring bespoke "damaged" 3D meshes, trauma events are injected into `#66 solve_homeostatic_graph_flux` and `#65 populate_allometric_vascular_c1` as graph/conductance perturbations:
1. **Severance / Amputation / Wind-Snap / Bushfire Pruning:** Cutting edge $u$ halts the downward auxin stream from the severed apex, immediately unlocking dormant **epicormic or axillary buds** below $u$ into vigorous vertical reiterations (coppicing / post-fire epicormic resprouting).
2. **Vascular Occlusion / Ischemia / Embolism ($K_{uv} \to 0$):** Clots, ringbarking, or drought xylem cavitation starve downstream tissue ($\text{O}_2 \downarrow$), spiking local hypoxia/VEGF attractors and elevated upstream wall pressure to trigger **collateralogenesis** (sprouting anastomosing bypass loops and dilating dormant micro-collaterals via Murray's Law $\alpha=3$).
3. **Mechanical Overload / Chronic Wind / Bone-Tendon Strain ($\boldsymbol{\sigma}$):** Persistent directional stress shifts `#65`'s cross-sectional populator from circular to eccentric elliptical cross-sections (**tension wood** on upper angiosperm limbs, **compression wood** on lower conifer limbs) or drives **Wolff's Law trabecular bone buttressing** (`#68`).
4. **Thermal / Fire Scar or Pathogen Canker (CODIT):** Partial circumferential necrosis on angular sector $[\theta_1, \theta_2]$ halts growth in the dead arc while adjacent living cambium hyper-proliferates radially into rolled **ram's-horn wound-wood callus ribs** (or fibrous scar encapsulation around a cyst/necrotic core).

### 8.5 Pillar 5 — The 4 Convergent Biophysical Bottlenecks & Diagnostic Signatures (Marcescence vs. Clean Drop)
Every edge and terminal organ in `BranchGraph10d` tracks **four convergent physiological state variables** bridging diverse abiotic and biotic stressors to geometric population:
1. **Water Potential & Turgor Pressure ($\Psi, P_{\text{turgor}}$):** Drives **Tier 1 (Minutes–Hours, Reversible)** elastic kinematics—**paraheliotropism** (pendulous *Eucalyptus* petiole torsion and bulliform grass leaf rolling parallel to solar EMF vectors), reversible Euler wilting ($E_{\text{eff}} \propto P_{\text{turgor}}$), and smooth-muscle vasodilation/vasoconstriction.
2. **Hydraulic Conductance ($K_{\text{hyd}} \in [0, 1]$):** Drives **Tier 2 (Days–Weeks, Hydraulic Segmentation)** when $\Psi < \Psi_{50}$ (xylem cavitation/embolism)—peripheral leaves and fine twigs act as **hydraulic fuses**, triggering sacrificial leaf shedding, summer branch drop (**cladoptosis**), and top-down **stag-heading**.
3. **Biochemical / Pigment State ($C_{\text{metab}}$):** Chlorophyll, anthocyanin, tannin/phenol, or inflammatory exudate concentrations driving `.m64` spectral shifts (chlorosis yellowing, frost/heat purpling, necrotic browning, or cutaneous erythema).
4. **Defense / Immune Signal ($I_{\text{def}}$):** Jasmonates/salicylic acid in flora or cytokines/histamines in fauna driving pressurized **kino/resin** exudation at bark-beetle galleries, cytokinin-hijacked **insect galls / witches' brooms** (SDF `smin` metaball blisters), or vascular endothelial permeability and interstitial `.10d` **edema**.
* **Emergent Diagnostic Realism (Clinging Marcescence vs. Clean Abscission Drop):** Comparing $\left|\frac{d P_{\text{turgor}}}{dt}\right|$ against corky **abscission-zone progress** automatically separates **slow drought / seasonal senescence** (clean leaf drop onto terrain litter, leaving bare twigs) from **acute radiant heat scorch or vascular wilt fungi (*Phytophthora* / Dutch Elm tyloses slamming $K_{\text{hyd}}\to 0$ in one root/stem sector)**, where tissue plasmolyzes before the abscission layer can form—leaving **asymmetric rust-brown curled leaves frozen onto the dead branch (*marcescence / flagging*)**.

### 8.6 Pillar 6 — Two-Tier Architecture: Regime A Macro-Scaffold vs. Regime B High-Multiplicity Phyllotactic / Acinar Terminal Amplifier
* **Why Current Trees & Organs Look Sparse (`botanical_anatomy.rs:1725-1775`, `whitegum_foliage.rs:6-100`, `human_welfare_anatomy.rs:822-833`):**
  - `botanical_anatomy.rs:1733` spawns a hardcoded `num_leaves = 7` per twig (or `whitegum_foliage.rs` replaces shoots with scalloped ellipsoid blobs, flagged in `ecopunk-graphics-audit` **G04/G05**), while `human_welfare_anatomy.rs:822` models lungs as 2 bare ellipsoids.
  - A biological tree is **not** a single-regime binary fractal all the way down: **Regime A (Macro-Scaffold)** bifurcates sparsely ($1\to 2$ or $3$) via Space Colonization/CCO to minimize hydraulic drag, whereas at the outer periphery it switches to **Regime B (Deterministic High-Multiplicity Meristematic / Acinar Packing)** where a single terminal axis spawns $8$ to $400+$ lateral organs in millimetres.
* **The Universal Vogel / Bravais-Bravais Terminal Amplifier (`#67 hypermedia.manifold.amplify_terminal_phyllotaxis`):**
  - For the $k$-th lateral organ ($k = 0, 1, \dots, N-1$) along a terminal shoot, floral receptacle, or acinar ductule:
    $$\theta_k = k \cdot \phi, \qquad z_k = \sum_{j=0}^k L(j), \qquad r_k = R(z_k)$$
    governed by **Divergence Angle $\phi$ & Whorl Multiplicity $M$** ($\phi \approx 137.508^\circ$ golden spiral, $\phi = 180^\circ$ distichous planar sprays, $M=2$ opposite-decussate, $M=3\text{--}8+$ whorled), **Internode Length Function $L(j)$**, and **Acrotony vs. Basitony**:
    - **$L(j) > 0$:** Elongated leafy twigs, racemes, or conifer needle shoots.
    - **$L(j) \to 0$ (Internode Compression):** Nodes telescope into a single apex, turning the same closed-form equation into a high-multiplicity burst ($1 \to 7..400+$): *Eucalyptus* **umbels** (3, 7, 11, 15 operculate flower buds), *Corymbia* **corymbose panicles**, *Callistemon*/*Banksia* cylindrical **spikes**, *Acacia* globular wattle **capitula**, **bipinnate compound leaves** ($1 \text{ petiole} \to 12 \text{ pinnae} \to 40 \text{ pinnules} = 480 \text{ leaflets/node}$), and in fauna **pulmonary alveolar acini**, **anastomosing capillary beds**, **hepatic lobules**, and **renal glomeruli**.
  - **Architectural Payoff for `.q42`, `.10d`, and Etalon GPU:**
    1. **Coarse `.q42` Simulation Graph:** QualiaDB tracks only Order 3–4 structural branches ($200\text{--}1,500$ nodes per tree), solving flux, wind bending, cavitation, and fire/insect states cheaply at the shoot-cluster level.
    2. **Parallel Etalon WGSL Compute Expansion (Close LoD) & Analytic `SGGX` Collapse (Far LoD):** Each terminal `.q42` node carries a compact **Terminal Amplifier Descriptor** (`{ archetype, phi_rad, whorl_m, internode_l, sub_order_depth, organ_count, bloom_turgor_state }`). At close LoD, Etalon's megapixel-governed compute pipeline (`etalon-render/src/shaders/foliage_lod.wgsl`, extended beyond pasture grass culms to general Vogel whorls & anatomical acini) expands `organ_id = 0..N` in closed form with zero pointer chasing ($300 \text{ tips} \times 48 \text{ leaves} = 14,400 \text{ leaves}$). Beyond $80\text{ m}$, the exact same descriptor collapses analytically into `etalon-render/src/shaders/sggx_microflake.wgsl` (`REP_KIND_SGGX_CANOPY = 3`) whose anisotropic optical density, leaf-angle distribution, and heat-stressed porosity match the sum of its $N$ organs.

## 9. Qualia Anatomy Atlas (Production Wave-09) End-to-End Morphism & Runtime Audit

Tracing the four P0/P1 requirements from the Wave-09 medical-atlas handoffs ([`field-handoff.md`](file:///C:/github/anatomy/working/medical-atlas/production-wave-09/field-handoff.md), [`compound-review.md`](file:///C:/github/anatomy/working/medical-atlas/production-wave-09/compound/compound-review.md), and [`neutral-field-contact.json`](file:///C:/github/anatomy/working/medical-atlas/production-wave-09/compound/neutral-field-contact.json)) across **Synth / `asset-production-vibe`**, **sealed `.10d` / `.hmc` (`qualia-core-db::container_10d`)**, and **QualiaPortal (`qualia-core-db::render` + `etalon-render`)** explains every observed Wave-09 failure and defines the exact morphism and runtime remedies.

### 9.1 P0 · Preserve Anatomy in Field-Only Rendering (`0x0307 SDFB` vs. `0x0306 HOLO`)

#### Exact Mathematical & Code Root Cause of the Wave-09 Field Failures
1. **Producer Fit Collapse (`qualia-core-db/src/container_10d/holographic_phase_section.rs:683-918`):**
   - `upgrade_legacy_10d_to_holographic_v2` (`compile_10d.rs:1118`) invokes `fit_m64_holo_segments_from_mesh_zero_heap`, which projects the entire mesh onto a **single 2D $24 \times 24$ XZ grid (`G = 24`, `line 714`)** and caps output at `MAX_FITTED_HOLO_SEGMENTS = 64` (`line 644`).
   - **Single Y-Interval Collapse (`lines 735-777`):** For every triangle overlapping XZ cell `(ix, iz)`, it updates `cell_ymin[cid] = min(cell_ymin, tmin_y)` and `cell_ymax[cid] = max(cell_ymax, tmax_y)` — storing **only ONE vertical `[cell_ymin, cell_ymax]` interval per `(ix, iz)` column**. Any hollow Y gap between an upper wall and a lower wall inside the same `(ix, iz)` column is structurally unrepresentable.
   - **X Row-Run Convexification (`lines 796-838`):** Along each Z-row `iz`, `while ix < G && cell_tri_counts[iz * G + ix] > 0` (`line 814`) merges all contiguous occupied X cells into a single cuboid whose Y span is `[run_ymin, run_ymax]` (`min` of all `cell_ymin` to `max` of all `cell_ymax` across the entire X run, `lines 817-818`), padded by $+0.08 \cdot \Delta x$ and $0.58 \cdot \Delta z$ (`lines 834-835`).
2. **Visitor Cuboid Approximation (`qualia-core-db/src/render/portal_load_10d.rs:173-185, 325-410` & `render/portal/scene_mesh.rs:219-235`):**
   - Neither standalone (`decode_load_10d` $\to$ `portal_load_10d.rs:175`) nor scene assembly (`SceneMeshAccum::append_asset_10d` $\to$ `scene_mesh.rs:222`) performs SDF raymarching on `.10d` `HOLO` records (`PortalGpu`'s `holographic_standalone.wgsl:48-61, 280-316` is an RDF knowledge-graph primitive renderer). Both call `synthesize_holographic_segments_mesh`, which builds an **8-vertex axis-aligned cuboid (`box_tris`) per `M64HoloRecord`**, collapsing all 12 `sdf_harmonics` into a single scalar `mod_factor = clamp(sum, -0.10, +0.10)` scaling `rx` and `rz` while leaving `ry` unmodulated (`lines 370-391`).
3. **Why Each Wave-09 Failure Occurs:**
   - **Y-Aligned Tube (`36/36` pass) vs. $90^\circ$-Rotated X-Aligned Tube (`36/36` fail) vs. Cystic Duct (`104/104` fail) & Umbilical Vein (`44/44` fail):** When a tube runs vertically along **Y**, its annular wall projects onto XZ as a ring with an empty center (`cell_tri_counts == 0`), so the `while ix < G` row-run breaks at the hole (`lines 802-805`), leaving an open central canal. (C1 and C2 passed all `588` checks and `84` projections in Wave-08 only because cervical vertebral foramina run vertically along **Y**.) The moment the tube is rotated $90^\circ$ onto **X** (or follows the curved/oblique path of the cystic duct or helical umbilical vein), the top wall and bottom wall of the lumen project onto the **exact same XZ cell `(ix, iz)`**—collapsing `[cell_ymin, cell_ymax]` to `[-R_{\text{outer}}, +R_{\text{outer}}]` and sealing the lumen completely shut with a solid Y-spanning cuboid!
   - **Adult Spleen (`+42.62%–42.64%` volume inflation: `95.255 cm³` $\to$ `135.874 cm³`, erasing visceral hilum `0.686 cm` $\to$ `-0.141 cm` and marginal notch `0.576 cm` $\to$ `0 cm`):** Merging contiguous X cells into `[run_ymin, run_ymax]` extrudes every Z cross-section into an axis-aligned Y-bounding slab, completely filling the concave visceral hilum and marginal notch (`closed-organ-review.md:14`).

#### Sealing `mesh_to_sdf_brick` (`Q42SDF16`) into `.10d` (`FIELD_KIND_SIGNED_DISTANCE_BRICK = 0x0307` `SDFB`)
* **Proven Accuracy (`organ/sdf-diagnostic.json:6-75`):** A standalone $32^3$ `Q42SDF16` brick from `69 hypermedia.manifold.mesh_to_sdf_brick` achieved **`94.962 cm³` (`-0.307%` volume error)**, **minimum section IoU `0.9866`** across all 10 planes, **`0.669 / 0.686 cm` hilum depth**, and **`0.407 / 0.576 cm` notch depth**.
* **Tag Allocation & Byte-Budget Architecture (`manifold_identity_v2.rs:66-86`):**
  - Because `0x0305` is already assigned to `FIELD_KIND_SYMBOLIC_RDF_CBOR_LD` (`CBLD`) and `0x0306` is `HOLO`, allocate **`FIELD_KIND_SIGNED_DISTANCE_BRICK = 0x0307` (`SDFB`)**.
  - Upgrade `mesh_to_sdf_brick` (`asset-production-vibe/src/manifold_fields_sdf/mod.rs:237-348`) from brute-force $O(N^3 \cdot T)$ dense `f32` ($32^3$ cap) to **BVH-accelerated $32^3..128^3$ with sparse narrow-band $8^3$ `i16`/`f16` bricks (`|\phi| \le 3 \Delta x$, `1 KiB/brick`) + coarse 1-bit occupancy bitmask + local $2\times$ refinement bricks around thin ducts/lumens (`1–2 mm` cystic duct)**.
  - **Budget Fit:** Typical single-organ `.10d` `SDFB` payloads remain **`100–600 KiB`** (dense $32^3$ `f32` = `128 KiB`, dense $64^3$ `f16` = `512 KiB`), fitting well within `preview` (`16 MiB` resource / `64 MiB` aggregate) and `production16` (`128 MiB` resource / `2048 MiB` aggregate), and rendering rotationally invariant iso-surfaces in QualiaPortal via GPU 3D-texture sphere-tracing or Dual Contouring.

---

### 9.2 P0 · Seal Assemblies from Independently Authored Leaves (`AREF` / `IOVR` / `INST`)

* **What Already Exists vs. What Is Unwired:**
  - `qualia-core-db/src/container_10d/archetype_ref_section.rs:26-500` already implements the binary codecs for `AREF` (`0x0301`, `80 B` `ArchetypeRefRecord` with `Sha256=2` / `Blake3_256=1` 32-byte digest), `IOVR` (`0x0302`, `48 B` `InstanceOverrideHeader` carrying `uniform_scale: f32` and full 3D PGA rigid pose `local_pga_motor: [f32; 8]`), and `INST` (`0x0303`, `64 B` `InstanceBatchRecord`).
  - **Authoring Gap:** Morphism `123` (`hypermedia.manifold.sweep_assembly_profile`, `manifold_assembly_bim_erosion.rs:16-380`), `compile_10d`, and `compile_10d_pbr` flatten geometry into a single inline `QuantizedMesh`. `compile_computational_instance_to_10d` (`compile_10d.rs:892-990`) writes at most one `AREF` and has zero parameters for `IOVR` (`0x0302`), `INST` (`0x0303`), or multi-leaf manifests.
  - **Visitor Runtime Gap:** Across all of `qualia-core-db/src/render/`, there are **zero callers** of `decode_archetype_ref_record`, `InstanceOverrideHeader`, or `InstanceBatchHeader`. `decode_load_10d` (`portal_load_10d.rs:94-137`) and `SceneMeshAccum::append_asset_10d` (`scene_mesh.rs:155-200`) scan `ManifoldIdentityV2` exclusively for `HOLO` (`0x0306`), forcing Wave-09's `compound-field.mjs` (`compound-review.md:7-9`) to perform SHA-256 verification (`fetchVerified`), `.10d` binary parsing (`readField`), rigid-transform math (`transform`), and per-frame vertex mutation (`portal.write_part_vertices`) in JavaScript.
* **Remedy (`#80 hypermedia.manifold.seal_leaf_assembly_10d` + QualiaPortal `admit_leaf_assembly`):**
  - Authoring morphism `#80` seals external leaf URIs, SHA-256 digests, `IOVR` 8-float PGA motors, and `INST` batches into a meshless compound `.10d` / `.hmc` with zero duplicated leaf vertices.
  - QualiaPortal's `admit_leaf_assembly` decodes `AREF`+`IOVR`+`INST`, fetches each referenced leaf `.10d`, verifies its SHA-256 digest **fail-closed** (rejecting missing or 1-bit-tampered leaves without corrupting the active scene), and uploads `IOVR.local_pga_motor` directly into `PortalGpu`'s existing deduplicated `MeshInstanceGroup`s (`instance_group_upload.rs:6-60`) for both surface and `SDFB`/`HOLO` field-only paths.

### 9.3 P1 · Fit and Verify Articulation Using Anatomical Landmarks

* **Why Current Operations Cannot Produce the Wave-09 Cervical Receipt:**
  - `147 query_anatomical_ontology_pin` (`rig_ontology_continuum_frustum.rs:300-366`) queries a static 32-pin table (`CANONICAL_ANATOMICAL_PINS`, `scale 4..5`) rather than authored `.10d`/`CBLD` landmarks.
  - `146 rig_biomechanical_archetype` (`lines 28-281`) builds a fixed 24-joint `SKL1` fauna template without articular facet surfaces or ligament centroids.
  - `74 step_island_xpbd` and `75 query_direct_space` (`motor_xpbd_space.rs:78-400`) only test **coarse spheres and axis-aligned bounding boxes (`ray_intersect_aabb`, `line 343`)**, making concave atlantoaxial odontoid-fovea facet clearance and spinal-canal encroachment unmeasurable.
* **Remedy (`#81 hypermedia.kinetics.fit_and_verify_articulation_sweep`):**
  - Fits resting `IOVR` `local_pga_motor` `[f32; 8]` and `SKL2` ROM limits from paired `Uberon`/`FMA` attachment sites and articular facets via Kabsch/axis-fit.
  - Evaluates exact SDF (`SDFB` `0x0307`) and triangle-BVH signed clearance across sampled pose trajectories (`neutral`, `flexion ±1°`, `rotation ±12°`), partitioning contacts into **`allowed_contact_zones`** (e.g., atlantoaxial lateral facets & fovea dentis ↔ dens within cartilage allowance `[0, +cartilage_gap_mm]`), **`illegal_penetration`** (extra-articular bone overlap count & volume `cm³`), and **`protected_void_canals`** (minimum clearance `mm` to the vertebral foramen / spinal cord).
  - Emits a deterministic JSON + `.q42` receipt with strict epistemic separation: `"engineering_geometry_status": "pass|fail"` vs. `"anatomical_review_status": "pending_clinical_review|approved_by:<DID>"` (never auto-approving anatomical sign-off).

---

### 9.4 P1 · Make Interactive Cutaways Retain Anatomical Identity

* **Why `78 bisect_plane` and QualiaPortal Currently Lose Wall-vs-Lumen Identity:**
  - `78 hypermedia.manifold.bisect_plane` (`solidify_bisect_subdivide.rs:83-260`) is a destructive single-mesh CPU slicer that strips `MAT1`/`UV01`/`CBLD`/semantic IDs (`attribute_policy="discard"`) and caps the cut boundary with a **single centroid triangle fan (`lines 215-260`)**—which stitches solid triangles straight across open hollow lumens (like the cystic duct) and foramina!
  - `LoupeDissectionState::cutaway_plane_01` (`biological_ontology.rs:145`) is an unrendered CPU struct field; neither `mesh.wgsl` nor `holographic_standalone.wgsl` has a section-plane uniform, and `picking.rs:1-55` returns only a single per-instance `u64` `semantic_id`.
* **Remedy (`#82 hypermedia.manifold.configure_interactive_cutaway` + `PortalGpu::set_section_plane`):**
  1. **Repair Static `bisect_plane`:** Replace the centroid triangle fan with **hole-aware even-odd / constrained-Delaunay loop triangulation** so annular walls remain open rings, and forward `SubmeshRange`, `SurfaceReading`, and `.q42` semantic IDs across split edges.
  2. **Live Non-Destructive Cutaway Across Surface, `SDFB` Field, and Streamed `AREF` Leaves:** Bind a shared 64-byte `CutawaySectionPlane` UBO (`plane_abcd`, `slab_thickness_m`, `affected_leaf_mask`) in `PortalGpu` across `mesh.wgsl` and the `SDFB`/`HOLO` shader. On the section plane, evaluate the leaf's signed distance field $\phi(\mathbf{x})$:
     - **$\phi(\mathbf{x}) \le 0$ (Cut Tissue Wall):** Shades solid cut-wall cross-section and tags GPU picking (`picking.rs`) with `surface_region = "cut_tissue_wall"` and `wall_ontology_iri` (e.g., `UBERON:0001152` cystic duct wall).
     - **$\phi(\mathbf{x}) > 0$ Inside Enclosed Contour (Internal Cavity / Lumen):** Shades recessed/translucent lumen void and tags picking with `surface_region = "internal_cavity_lumen"` and `lumen_ontology_iri` (e.g., cystic duct lumen or `vertebral_foramen`), enabling VibeScript to label, highlight, and point to walls and lumens independently.

### 9.5 Wave-09 Anatomy Audit Deliverable Matrix

## 11. Deep Dive: Digital Twin to Physical Fabrication & 3D Printing Pipeline (Stages 1–5)

### 11.1 Discovery: Existing Print/Export Kernels in `qualia-core-db` With Zero `.vibe` or MCP Callers

Auditing [`WIP/README.md:111`](file:///c:/github/WIP/README.md#L111) against `qualia-core-db/src/specialized_libs/computer_vision/spatial/` and `computational_geometry/` reveals that **all physical fabrication, `.3mf`, `.stl`, manifold-qualification, and plane-slicing primitives reside in `qualia-core-db`, and zero (`0 / 150`) morphisms in `hypermedia-production` expose any of them**:

1. **`qualia-core-db/src/specialized_libs/computer_vision/spatial/export_3mf.rs:62-238` (`mesh_ir_to_3mf` — `0` callers in `hypermedia-production` or MCP):**
   - Pure-Rust ZIP writer (`STORED` uncompressed method with IEEE CRC-32) emitting `[Content_Types].xml`, `_rels/.rels`, and a 3MF Core XML `<model unit="millimeter">` with a single `<object id="1" type="model">`.
   - **Current Limitations (`Category B`):** Lacks the **3MF Production Extension** (multi-part UUID assemblies and component transform matrices), **Materials & Properties Extension** (multi-color / multi-extruder / graded material LUTs), **Slice Extension** (SLA/SLS 2D contour stacks), Deflate compression, and embedded `/Metadata/qualia_provenance.q42` (`CBLD` + `source-asset-licensing.n3` receipt).
2. **`spatial/export_stl.rs:7-69` (`mesh_ir_to_stl_binary` — `0` callers in `hypermedia-production` or MCP):**
   - Pure-Rust binary STL writer (80-byte header + triangle count + 50-byte facet records).
3. **`spatial/print_readiness.rs:7-60` (`PrintReadiness` — `0` callers in `hypermedia-production` or MCP):**
   - Returns `PrintReadiness { ok, manifold_ok, triangle_count, bounds_mm, within_envelope, note }`, where `note` explicitly states: **`"print-ready heuristic: validated mesh within envelope (not guaranteed slicer success)"`**.
   - **Critical Gap (`Category B`):** `print_readiness.rs` sets `manifold_ok` solely from `validate_mesh_ir` (non-empty, finite coordinates, in-bounds triangle indices) + AABB vs. `envelope_mm`—it **never calls** `qualify_surface_topology_into` or `self_intersecting_pairs`!
4. **`spatial/twin_bridge.rs:1-100` (`closed_form_bar_stretch`):**
   - Evaluates only a 1D analytical bar formula $\delta = FL/(AE)$ (`"**Not** a mesh FEA solve"`), uncoupled from 3D tetrahedral FEM (`computational_geometry/fem_certificate.rs`).
5. **Unwired Exact Computational-Geometry Primitives in `qualia-core-db/src/specialized_libs/computational_geometry/` (`Category A`):**
   - `surface_topology_qualification.rs:17-100` (`qualify_surface_topology_into`): Zero-heap verification that an indexed triangle mesh is a closed 2-manifold with **0 boundary edges**, oppositely oriented paired half-edges, 1 connected component, and 1 closed vertex fan per vertex.
   - `surface_mesh_processing.rs:1-94` (`surface_area`, `signed_volume` $\sum \frac{1}{6} \mathbf{a}\cdot(\mathbf{b}\times\mathbf{c})$ verifying outward CCW orientation).
   - `tri_tri_3.rs:1-85` (`self_intersecting_pairs` using exact `orient_3d` predicates + BVH).
   - `corefine_3d.rs:89-100` & `boolean_3.rs`: Exact 3D mesh co-refinement and boolean CSG within the 42 MiB Sentinel budget (`COREFINE_BUDGET_BYTES`).
   - `medial_axis_3d.rs:15-100`: 3D Voronoi-pole Medial Axis Transform (`MedialSample { pole, radius, outer }`), whose docstring explicitly notes `"medial radius — enough to seed ... volumetric thickness fields"`.
   - `mesh_plane_slice.rs:1-100` (`slice_mesh_with_plane`): Intersects a triangle mesh with an arbitrary plane, emitting raw unlinked `[Point3; 2]` segments (without closed polygon contour chaining, hole nesting trees, or perimeter offsetting).
   - `space_colonization.rs:24-100` (`space_colonization_3d`): 3D branching skeleton generator—ready to serve as the backend for organic/tree 3D-printing supports!

### 11.2 Five-Stage Digital-Twin-to-Physical-Part Fabrication Architecture (Morphisms `#91–#96`)

```
Digital Twin (.hmc / .10d v2 with AREF/IOVR/INST, .m64 matter, FEMC stress, .q42 BOM & CBLD license)
   │
   ├─► Stage 1 (#91 certify_dfm_manifold):
   │     qualify_surface_topology_into (0 boundary edges) + signed_volume + self_intersecting_pairs (orient_3d)
   │     + medial_axis_3d min-wall thickness audit + overhang (>45°) & bridge scan + SLA trapped-resin cavity BFS
   │
   ├─► Stage 2 (#92 split_joinery_assembly):
   │     Decompose oversized twin / AREF assembly into bed-bounded parts via corefine_3d / boolean_3
   │     + boolean interlocking joinery (SlidingDovetail | TaperedDowelPin | SnapFit | ISO M-Series Heat-Set Boss)
   │     + apply material shrinkage & kerf clearance offset (Δ ∈ [0.05, 0.30] mm) + emboss .q42 BOM part serials
   │
   ├─► Stage 3 (#93 synth_tpms_lattice):
   │     Offset-shell hollowing + gravity-routed SLA resin drain/air-relief ports
   │     + FEMC von-Mises stress-graded TPMS metamaterial infill (Gyroid / Schwarz-P / Diamond / Voronoi struts)
   │
   ├─► Stage 4 (#94 orient_nest_tree_support):
   │     Multi-objective orientation optimizer (protect cosmetic/articular/datum faces, minimize Z & SLA peel force)
   │     + 2D/3D No-Fit-Polygon build-plate nesting + Space-Colonization branching tree supports with breakaway tips
   │
   └─► Stage 5 (#95 slice_and_toolpath & #96 export_print_package):
         • #95: Chain slice_mesh_with_plane segments into nested outer/hole polygon trees -> 2D minkowski_2 offsets
                -> adaptive Z-cusp FFF G-code (Klipper/Marlin/Prusa/Bambu) or anti-aliased SLA/MSLA PNG slice stack
         • #96: Package into full 3MF (Core + Production UUID transforms + Materials + Slice Extensions)
                with embedded /Metadata/qualia_provenance.q42 (CBLD + source-asset-licensing.n3 receipt) or binary STL
```

| Stage & Proposed Morphism | Existing `qualia-core-db` Owners Wired (`Cat A/B`) | New Fabrication Kernel Required (`Cat C`) | Digital-Twin-to-Physical Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Stage 1: DFM & Printability Certification**<br>`#91 hypermedia.fabrication.certify_dfm_manifold` | `surface_topology_qualification::qualify_surface_topology_into`, `surface_mesh_processing::signed_volume`, `tri_tri_3::self_intersecting_pairs`, `medial_axis_3d`, `spatial::print_readiness` | Medial-radius / SDF minimum wall-thickness & thin-pin histogram vs. nozzle/laser spot size; overhang angle ($\theta > 45^\circ$) & unsupported bridge span detector; 3D flood-fill trapped-resin / unvented cavity detector. | Deterministic `DfmPrintCertificate` + per-face violation mask (non-manifold edges, self-intersections, thin walls, overhangs, trapped cavities). |

## 12. Deep Dive: H.265 / HEVC (`hvc1` / `hev1` / Main 10 `P010`) & Hardware-Accelerated Video Encode/Decode Pipeline

### 12.1 What `chronicle.mux_mp4`, `media-isobmff`, and `etalon-hal` Actually Do Today

A source-level trace across `hypermedia-production/crates/{asset-production-vibe, media-isobmff, video-h264-codec, video-h264-preview, video-prores-codec, bitstream-codec, chronicle-broadcast-containers}`, `qualiaDB/crates/qualia-geospatial`, and `etalon/etalon-hal` reveals a striking contrast between the container/GPU-surface layer (which already anticipates H.265/HEVC and 10-bit `P010`) and the codec/queue layer (which is completely missing H.265 and hardware video queues):

1. **`hypermedia.chronicle.mux_mp4` (`chronicle_ops.rs:29-111, 465-590`) Emits Uncompressed `I_PCM` H.264 Baseline Macroblocks:**
   - There is **no H.264 encoder crate** in `hypermedia-production` (`video-h264-codec` is a decode-only 8-bit H.264 parser).
   - Instead, `mux_mp4` writes H.264 Baseline (`profile_idc = 66`, Level `1.0`, 8-bit 4:2:0) where **every $16\times 16$ macroblock is serialized as raw uncompressed `I_PCM` (`mb_type = 25`, 384 raw YCbCr bytes per macroblock)** inside all-IDR (`0x65`) I-slices (`slice_type = 7`)—with zero DCT/quantization, zero intra/inter prediction, zero CABAC, and hard caps of **`256×256` max resolution, `<= 64` frames, 8-bit BT.709 only, no audio track muxing, and `<= 2 MiB` file size** (`video-h264-preview/src/lib.rs:202-292`).
   - Meanwhile, `video-h264-preview/src/movie.rs:218-266` (`Movie` decoder) supports only 8-bit H.264 and Apple ProRes (`video-prores-codec`) and **explicitly returns `Codec::Unsupported(...)` for H.265/HEVC, AV1, VP9, and 10-bit H.264**.
2. **`media-isobmff` Already Implements H.265 / HEVC (`hvc1` / `hev1` / `hvcC`) + HDR10 (`mdcv` / `clli` / `nclx`) (`Category A` Unwired):**
   - `hypermedia-production/crates/media-isobmff/src/codec.rs:303-424` already implements `HevcNalArray` and `HevcConfig` (`hvcC`, ISO/IEC 14496-15 §8.3.3) with full `parse()`/`to_bytes()`, `vps()` (NAL 32), `sps()` (NAL 33), `pps()` (NAL 34), chroma format, 8/10/12-bit luma/chroma depth, and profile/tier/level!
   - `codec.rs:1277-1283, 1377-1405` already implements `SampleEntry::hevc(cfg, w, h)` (`hvc1`/`hev1`) alongside HDR **`mdcv`** (`MasteringDisplay::bt2020`), **`clli`**, and **`colr` (`nclx`)**, verified in `media-isobmff/tests/{oracle_demux.rs:221, oracle_mux.rs:152}`.
   - Yet `chronicle_ops.rs:612-811` (`build_avc_mp4`) bypasses `media_isobmff::Mp4Writer` entirely and hand-rolls an `avc1`-only buffer, and `mux.rs:154-170` hardcodes `[b"isom", b"iso2", b"avc1", b"mp41", b"mp42"]` in `ftyp` instead of adding `hvc1`/`hev1` when an HEVC track is present.
3. **Geospatial Terrain Drape Expects Hardware HEVC / AV1 All-Intra (`qualiaDB/crates/qualia-geospatial/src/geotiff.rs:65`):**
   - `geotiff.rs:65` defines `CompressionCodec::Av1IntraDrape = 6` (*"Hardware-accelerated AV1 / HEVC all-intra video decode for visual drape"*), which currently fails with `UnsupportedCompression`.
4. **Etalon HAL Already Defines Zero-Copy Planar `NV12` (8-Bit) and `P010` (10-Bit) GPU Textures, But Disables Hardware Video Queues:**
   - `etalon-types/src/texture/format.rs:272, 289, 487-490` defines **`TextureFormat::NV12`** (8-bit 4:2:0: `R8Unorm` Plane 0 + `Rg8Unorm` Plane 1) and **`TextureFormat::P010`** (10-bit 4:2:0 for **HEVC Main 10 HDR**: `R16Unorm` Plane 0 + `Rg16Unorm` Plane 1).
   - `etalon-hal/src/dx12/mod.rs:973-1006` (`DXGI_FORMAT_NV12`/`P010` with `Texture::with_plane_slice`) and `etalon-hal/src/vulkan/conv.rs:80-81` (`G8_B8R8_2PLANE_420_UNORM` / `G10X6_B10X6R10X6_2PLANE_420_UNORM_3PACK16`) already map these multi-planar surfaces on Direct3D 12 and Vulkan!
   - **Why Hardware H.265 Silicon Cannot Run Yet:**
     - `etalon-hal/src/vulkan/command.rs:167` explicitly comments `// We don't use VK_KHR_video_encode_queue`, and `vulkan/adapter.rs:2485-2495` only selects `vk::QueueFlags::GRAPHICS`—never probing `VK_QUEUE_VIDEO_ENCODE_BIT_KHR` / `VK_QUEUE_VIDEO_DECODE_BIT_KHR` or enabling `VK_KHR_video_queue`, `VK_KHR_video_encode_h265`, or `VK_KHR_video_decode_h265`.
     - Zero platform hardware codec bindings exist for Direct3D 12 Video (`ID3D12VideoDevice` / Windows Media Foundation `IMFTransform`), Apple `VideoToolbox`, Linux `VA-API`, or `NVENC`/`AMF`/`QuickSync`.
     - `format.rs:1002-1005` restricts `NV12`/`P010` to `SAMPLED_IMAGE` (no WGSL compute shader `Rgba16Float` $\to$ `P010`/`NV12` write pass), and `Anvil` `.np64` (`NodeProfile64` in `etalon-resources/src/device_caps.rs:10-165`) does not yet probe hardware HEVC Main / Main 10 / 4:2:2 / 4:4:4 encode/decode capabilities.

### 12.2 Architectural Solution: Anvil-Attested Hardware H.265 Bridge + Zero-Copy `P010`/`NV12` Pipeline (`#9` & `#137`)

Rather than implementing a slow scalar CPU H.265 encoder in software, modern GPU/SoC media engines (NVIDIA NVENC, AMD VCN/AMF, Intel QuickSync, Apple VideoToolbox, Qualcomm Adreno/Hexagon) natively accelerate **H.265 / HEVC Main (8-bit `NV12`) and Main 10 (10-bit `P010` HDR10 / PQ / HLG)**:

| Layer | Component & File Target | Concrete Upgrade |
|---|---|---|
| **1. Anvil `.np64` Hardware Codec Probing** | `etalon-resources/src/device_caps.rs` & `NodeProfile64` | Add `VideoCodecCaps` flags probing hardware H.265/HEVC (`Main`, `Main10_P010`, `Main422_10`, `AllIntra`), AV1, and H.264 encode/decode queues and max resolution (4K/8K). |
| **2. Etalon Zero-Copy `Rgba16 <-> P010/NV12` & Video Queues** | `etalon-types/src/texture/format.rs`, `etalon-hal/src/{vulkan,dx12,metal}` | Enable `STORAGE_BINDING` / compute write on `NV12`/`P010` plane slices; add a `wgsl_forge`-certified color-conversion compute pass (`Rgba16` linear/PQ/HLG BT.2020 $\leftrightarrow$ `P010`/`NV12`, `VerifyCpuRefBool = true`); expose `VK_KHR_video_{encode,decode}_h265`, `D3D12Video` / Media Foundation hardware MFT, `VideoToolbox`, and `VA-API` behind an `etalon` `VideoCodecProvider`. |
| **3. 2-Byte HEVC NAL & Container Muxing** | `bitstream-codec/src/lib.rs`, `media-isobmff/src/mux.rs`, `chronicle-broadcast-containers` | Add 2-byte HEVC NAL header parser (`nal_unit_type` 32=`VPS`, 33=`SPS`, 34=`PPS`, 19/20=`IDR_W_RADL`/`IDR_N_LP`), wire `media_isobmff::SampleEntry::hevc` (`hvc1` + `hvcC` + `mdcv`/`clli`/`nclx`), and add Matroska `V_MPEGH/ISO/HEVC` and MPEG-TS `stream_type = 0x24`. |
| **4. Authoring Morphisms (`#9` & `#137`)** | `#9 hypermedia.chronicle.encode_video_master` & `#137 hypermedia.chronicle.decode_video_master` | Support `H265_HEVC_Main10_P010` (HDR10/4K/8K), `H265_HEVC_Main_NV12`, and `H265_HEVC_Intra` (unblocking `geotiff.rs:65` `.m64` terrain visual drape) with the two-tier determinism contract and decoder determinism asymmetry below. |

### 12.3 Settled Two-Tier Determinism Contract (`#9`) & Decoder Determinism Asymmetry (`#137`)

1. **`Determinism::HardwareFast_NP64` (Default for Interactive Preview, Viewport Capture & Bulk Production in `#9 encode_video_master`):**
   - Submits zero-copy `P010` (10-bit Main 10 HDR) or `NV12` (8-bit) Etalon GPU textures directly to `VK_KHR_video_encode_h265` / `D3D12Video` / `NVENC` / `VideoToolbox` queues (such as NVENC on RTX 5070 or RTX A2000).
   - Because vendor B-frame RDO, motion estimation, and CABAC engines are non-bit-exact across silicon revisions and driver versions, `#9` **MUST seal a dual-digest + perceptual quality gate receipt in `.q42` / `CBLD`**:
     - `source_frame_merkle_root`: BLAKE3/SHA-256 Merkle root of the pre-encode canonical `RadianceLattice` frames + `AudioMultiChannel` PCM samples (proving semantic/visual input equivalence across machines).
     - `encoded_bitstream_sha256`: SHA-256 of the emitted `hvc1` MP4/MOV/MKV container.
     - `anvil_np64_attestation`: The Anvil `.np64` device profile IRI (PCI `vendor:device`, driver version, rate-control mode, and Sanctuary isolation tier), plus a **minimum perceptual quality gate ($\text{PSNR}_Y \ge 42\text{ dB}$ or $\text{SSIM} \ge 0.985$ sampled on keyframe intervals)** so broken driver states fail closed.
2. **`Determinism::BitExactSoftware` (Mandatory for CI Regression Gates, Sanctuary Air-Gapped Reproducibility & Golden Masters in `#9`):**
   - Dispatches pure-Rust single-authority encoders with fixed thread-independent entropy framing—specifically pure-Rust Apple ProRes 422 / 4444 / XQ (`video-prores-codec`), deterministic intra/fixed-slice H.264 (`I_PCM`), or tile-wavefront-locked AV1—guaranteeing identical output container SHA-256 digests across x86_64 and ARM64 hosts without requiring GPU video silicon.
3. **Decoder Determinism Asymmetry (`#137 hypermedia.chronicle.decode_video_master`):**
   - Unlike encoding, **ITU-T H.265 / ISO/IEC 23008-2 decoding is mathematically deterministic at the YCbCr integer plane level** (normative integer inverse transform, intra/inter prediction, deblocking filter, and SAO).
   - Therefore, `#137 decode_video_master` uses hardware decode (`NVDEC` / `VK_KHR_video_decode_h265` / `D3D12Video` / `VideoToolbox` / `VA-API`) to write raw **`NV12` (`R8_UNorm + RG8_UNorm`)** or **`P010` (`R16_UNorm + RG16_UNorm`)** planes, while **chroma upsampling and BT.709 / BT.2020 / PQ / HLG color conversion MUST run through an Etalon-owned `wgsl_forge` compute shader (`VerifyCpuRefBool = true`)** rather than opaque vendor driver blitters—guaranteeing that hardware decoding remains bit-exact within **$\le 1\text{ ULP}$** of software decoding for downstream computer-vision and pose-extraction morphisms (`#52`, `#73`).

Because Gemini Carbon generated its review (`#97`–`#136` in Sections 4.3–4.5 above) without direct filesystem access to `C:\github\hypermedia-production`, `C:\github\qualiaDB`, `C:\github\etalon`, `C:\github\vibe-script`, and `C:\github\synth`, every recommendation was verified line-by-line against local Rust source.

The table below records the **12 Ground-Truth Codebase Errata** where Gemini Carbon assumed an encoder, compressor, or solver already existed locally, when in fact the local crate is decode-only, container-box-only, or an unimplemented trait stub. **Codex swarm workers MUST follow this Ground-Truth Errata Ledger** so Tier-1 wrapper agents do not call non-existent functions:

The actual bug is `asset-production-vibe/src/ontology/mod.rs:156-184, 316-318` hardcoding `PURE_COMPUTE` / `DeterministicExact` / `PureNoState` (`"Irreversible"` in JSON) across all 150 morphisms, plus `ParamSpec` (`descriptor.rs:232-241`) lacking QUDT unit IRI & domain-bound fields. | Assigned to **`WP-00` (Coordinator Gate)** to fix `ontology/mod.rs` and extend `ParamSpec` across both `qualiaDB` and `vibe-script`. |
