## 14. Deep Dive: Digital-to-Physical Steganographic & Creation-Tree Provenance Architecture (`#133`–`#136`)

Linking any exported 3D digital twin or physically fabricated object back to its authoritative QualiaDB / Webizen creation tree (`.vibe` morphism DAG, `.hmc` URI, `SI-01..12` contributor work-graph, `.m64` material lot, FEA safety certificate, and `source-asset-licensing.n3` Permissive Commons state) without relying on fragile stick-on QR labels uses a **Two-Tier Cryptographic Framing Protocol (`#133`)** paired with **Six Material-, Process- & Stress-Adaptive Carrier Modalities (`S1`–`S6` in `#134`–`#136`)**:

### 14.1 Two-Tier Creation-Tree Payload & Forward Error Correction (`#133 encode_creation_tree_payload`)
1. **Tier 1 — Compact Creation-Tree Anchor (`184 payload bits` $\to$ `256–512 ECC-coded channel symbols`):**
   - `sync_preamble` (`16 bits`): Barker / Gold autocorrelation sequence for blind spatial, angular, and scale lock during camera, audio, or 3D-scan readback.
   - `protocol_and_channel_id` (`8 bits`): Protocol version + active carrier modality hint (`S1`–`S6`).
   - `creation_tree_digest_128` (`128 bits`): Truncated BLAKE3 / SHA-256 content-addressed digest of the sealed `.hmc` / `.10d` `CBLD` creation-tree manifest (indexing the full `.vibe` morphism DAG, contributor `work-graph`, and `ML-DSA` signature in QualiaDB vaults).
   - `instance_bom_and_governance_flags` (`32 bits`): `AREF`/`IOVR` BOM part slot (`16 bits`) + governance bitmask (`8 bits`: clinical/structural safety-critical flag, Permissive Commons obligation-pool status, `TK/BC` custodial notice bit) + `CRC-16` (`8/16 bits`).
   - **Forward Error Correction (FEC):** Reed-Solomon $\text{RS}(64, 32)$ over $\text{GF}(2^8)$ or short-block LDPC / Polar codes with 2D spatial interleaving, recovering the exact anchor even when **$25\text{--}35\%$ of the physical object is sanded, UV-weathered, fractured, or occluded**.
2. **Tier 2 — High-Capacity Self-Contained CBOR-LD Receipt (`1 KiB` to `16 KiB`):** Automatically enabled whenever surface area, FFF slice count ($>500$ layers), or digital mesh vertex count ($>25,000$ vertices) provides sufficient channel capacity, embedding a compressed CBOR-LD provenance receipt and detached signature for 100% offline verification without vault network lookup.

### 14.2 Six Material-, Process- & Stress-Adaptive Carrier Modalities (`#134` & `#135`)

| Carrier Modality | Target Medium & Process | Physical / Mathematical Encoding Mechanism | Software / Companion Readback (`#136`) | Metrological & Mechanical Impact |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`S1` · Laplace-Beltrami Spectral & `SDFB` Narrow-Band Watermarking** (`#134`) | Digital `.10d`, `.glb`, `.obj`, `.stl`, `.3mf` meshes & `0x0307 SDFB` fields | Projects mesh onto mid-band Laplace-Beltrami eigenfunctions (`ddg_operators`) or `SDFB` narrow-band phase ($\pm 2\text{--}8\,\mu\text{m}$ normal displacement), clamped to **`0.0 µm` on `ProtectedFaceMasks`** (articular facets, threads, sealing datums). | Mesh/SDF spectral correlation against the Laplace-Beltrami basis (or blind spherical-harmonic phase lock). | Survives `.stl`/`.obj` metadata stripping, rigid transforms, uniform scaling, vertex reordering, and moderate decimation; `0%` DFM/fit impact. |
| **`S2` · Toolpath Layer-Thickness (*LayerCode*) & Thermal-Gloss Spread-Spectrum** (`#135`) | FFF / FDM Extrusion (`PLA`, `PETG`, `ASA`, `ABS`, `TPU`) | Integrated into `#95 slice_and_toolpath`: modulates adaptive Z layer height ($\pm 15\text{--}30\,\mu\text{m}$ differential bands) and/or outer-wall print-head feedrate & fan speed (creating matte/gloss polymer crystallinity bands without dimensional change) + Z-seam azimuthal phase codes. | Smartphone macro burst (`qualia-vision`) using 1D profile ridges + FFT demodulation under grazing ambient light. | Zero internal voids required; works on thin single-wall shells; survives painting (height mode) or handling (gloss/seam mode). |
| **`S3` · Subsurface Dielectric Void (*AirCode*) Scattering Matrix** (`#135`) | Semi-translucent / Unpigmented or White/Natural `PLA`, `PETG`, `SLA` Resin, `SLS PA12` | Integrated into `#93 synth_tpms_lattice`: places $0.5\text{--}1.0\text{ mm}$ subsurface air pockets or septum thickness modulations $0.6\text{--}1.2\text{ mm}$ beneath a smooth, unmarked outer shell; **gated by `FEMC` stress ($\sigma < 0.25\,\sigma_{\text{yield}}$)** and routed to SLA drain channels. | Invisible under diffuse room light; decoded via `qualia-vision` structured-light global-direct separation, $850\text{--}940\text{ nm}$ NIR transillumination, or CT (`#70`). | Completely hidden from naked eye; zero external surface roughness (ideal for clinical/hygienic/aerodynamic shells); survives surface sanding. |
| **`S4` · Micro-Facet BRDF Glint & Anisotropic Raster Polarization** (`#135`) | High-res `SLA`/`MSLA` ($20\text{--}50\,\mu\text{m}$), `PolyJet`, & 3/5-Axis `CNC` Finishing (`#124`) | Tilts microscopic surface micro-facets ($\pm 1.5^\circ\text{--}3.0^\circ$) on non-functional matte patches or alternates top-skin toolpath raster orientation ($0^\circ/45^\circ/90^\circ/135^\circ$) across $0.4\text{ mm}$ micro-cells. | Smartphone 2-frame photometric capture (flash-on vs. flash-off angular glint ratio in `qualia-vision`). | Zero internal volume required (works on $1\text{ mm}$ thin wafers and solid CNC metal parts); $>1,000\text{ bits/cm}^2$ on MSLA. |
| **`S5` · Vibro-Acoustic `TPMS` Eigenmode Resonance Ratios** (`#135`) | Solid / Latticed Structural Parts (`FFF`, `SLA`, `SLS`, Metal `DMLS`) | Uses `lanczos_eigensolver` (`#109`) + `elastic_virieux` (`#2`) to perturb internal `TPMS` Gyroid strut thickness distribution ($\pm 3\%$ local mass-neutral), tuning scale- and temperature-invariant eigenfrequency ratios $(f_2/f_1, f_3/f_1, \dots, f_{10}/f_1)$. | Tap object gently or sweep a $2\text{--}20\text{ kHz}$ phone-speaker chirp; `qualia-audio` CQT modal peak extractor decodes ratios in `200 ms`. | Zero visual or surface marks; works on carbon-black opaque parts; **simultaneously detects internal cracks, delamination, or counterfeit infill**. |
| **`S6` · Chipless RF / Dielectric Cavity Notch Signature** (`#135`) | Outdoor Civics Hardware, Sealed Enclosures, Embedded `ECAD` Parts (`#125`) | Uses `#1 solve_maxwell_yee_3d` to tune internal dielectric permittivity contrast ($20\%$ vs. $100\%$ infill slots) or embedded conductive trace split-ring resonators, creating a multi-notch RF backscatter spectrum ($2.4\text{--}24\text{ GHz}$ or `NFC` harmonic). | Handheld FMCW / UWB / Wi-Fi CSI reader (`#113` / `#136`) sweeping return-loss notches through mud, paint, or enclosure walls. | Zero battery or silicon IC required; immune to UV weathering, opaque paint coats, dirt, and surface abrasion. |

## 15. Codex Swarm & Work-Packet Implementation Plan (`WP-00`–`WP-25`)

This section provides the complete **Codex Swarm Execution Plan** designed so an external Codex agent fleet can execute the vast majority of the implementation across **26 disjoint work packets (`WP-00`–`WP-25`)** without file-merge collisions, followed by our quantitative post-implementation audit.

### 15.1 Execution DAG & Model-Tier Routing (Cost vs. Complexity)

- **Tier 1 (`T1` — Fast / Low-Cost Codex Workers):** Pure `Category A` Rust-to-`.vibe` wrapper wiring and Wave-13 fixture-to-real-input replacements where the underlying Rust crate functions already exist and pass unit tests.
- **Tier 2 (`T2` — Standard Coding Codex Workers):** Multi-crate pipelines, `.10d`/`.3mf`/`.hmc` binary section serialization, and wiring orphaned WGSL shaders into `PortalGpu` / `WaterGpu`.
- **Tier 3 (`T3` — High-Reasoning / Math-Specialist Codex Workers & Coordinator):** Shared ABI/handle coordinator gates (`WP-00`, `WP-25`) and genuine `Category C` numerical/graphics/HAL kernels (3D APIC/FLIP breaking waves, Tessendorf FFT + refractive caustics, Dual-Tree CCO & Borchert-Honda flux, 4D Kerr black-hole raymarching, Vulkan/D3D12 H.265 hardware video queues, OpenEXR/JXL/Opus/BC7 encoders, TPMS/joinery/slicing/CNC CAM, reverse-mode Wengert `ReverseTape`, and `S1`–`S6` steganographic modulators).

```
Phase 0 (Serial Coordinator Gate - T3):
  [WP-00: 16-Variant Artifact Enum, vibescript-abi ParamSpec/EffectSet Fix, 0x0307..0x030A Tags & Slot Reservations]
       │
       ├──► Phase 1 (Parallel Engine & Codec Crates — 100% Disjoint Crate/Module Trees):
```

### 15.2 Mandatory Escalation Protocol for Codex Workers ("When to Pause and Ask Timothy")

Every Codex worker brief MUST include the following **5 Mandatory Escalation Rules** (when triggered, the worker must **stop immediately and ask the user**, who can consult Antigravity for the exact resolution rather than letting Codex guess):
1. **Cross-Crate Binary Layout or FFI ABI Mutation:** If a worker believes it needs to alter an existing `#[repr(C)]` struct in `vibescript-abi` or change the byte layout of an existing `.10d` (`0x0200..0x0306`), `.q42`, `.hmc` (`QBDL`), `.m64`, `.emf`, or `.p64` header (rather than using the new tags reserved by `WP-00`).
2. **42 MiB Prolog Sentinel or Zero-Heap Violation:** If a symbolic `.q42` or `FixedArena` hot-path requires dynamic heap allocation or exceeds the 42 MiB arena cap (or if a `.10d` asset exceeds `production16`'s 128 MiB per-ring budget).
3. **External Dependency or License Conflict:** Before adding **any** external Cargo dependency to `Cargo.toml` (especially any C/C++ FFI wrapper or GPL/LGPL/MPL crate that could taint `CC BY-NC-ND 4.0` provenance in `qualiaDB`, `hypermedia-production`, or `synth`, or cross the `C:\github\chora` MPL-2.0 process boundary).
4. **Hardware Queue / Driver Unavailability (`WP-09` H.265 & `WP-05` GPU):** If a hardware queue (`VK_KHR_video_encode_h265`, `D3D12Video`, `VideoToolbox`) is unavailable on the test host—workers must **never** spoof an `hvc1` container with H.264 `I_PCM` bytes or silently downgrade precision to pass a test.
5. **Ambiguous Scientific / Clinical Invariant (`Wave-09` & `WellFair`):** If a mesh or organ fixture fails a `synth_validate_model` gate ($\phi_{\text{cavity}} \le -\epsilon$, $d \in [0,1]$, `enthesis` drift $\le 10^{-4}\text{ m}$, or Wave-09 lumen preservation)—workers must **never** weaken or bypass the validator threshold to make a test green.

## 17. Settled Architectural Verdicts & Phased Execution Sequence (Decisions 1–25)

All 25 design questions across `synth`, `hypermedia-production`, `qualiaDB`, and `etalon` are now settled into the normative engineering verdicts and six-phase rollout sequence below.

### 17.1 Resolved Architectural Verdicts (Decisions 1–25)

### 17.3 Mandatory `.hmc` Embedded `.q42` Semantic Model & `.10d` Bidirectional Addressability Invariant (`WP-01`, `WP-05`, `WP-08`, `WP-17`, `WP-25`)

In the `.hmc` (`QBDL`, `qualia-core-db/src/bundle/format.rs`) architecture, **the full semantic model MUST be packaged as an authoritative `.q42` vault entry (`kind = "q42"`, e.g. `semantic_model.q42` / `manifest.q42`) inside the `.hmc` container**, while the sibling `.10d`, `.m64`, `.emf`, and `.qac` entries carry deterministic 64-bit `q_hash` (`semantic_id`) pointers into that `.q42` vault. Because `.hmc` stores every embedded file page-aligned (`64 B` / `4096 B`) and intact, `BundleReader::get("semantic_model.q42")` yields a zero-copy slice that the `.q42` reader (`Q42LEX` + 6-column `NQuin` index + SLG-VM / N3Logic / Prolog `FixedArena` + SHACL) queries directly in memory without unpacking:
1. **Level 1 — Dedicated `.q42` Semantic Model Entry Inside Every `.hmc` Container (`#115 pack_and_diff_hmc` & `synth_produce_present`):** Every sealed `.hmc` (`QBDL`) bundle contains a primary `.q42` entry (`kind = "q42"`, `"semantic_model.q42"`) holding: (a) the bijective **`Q42LEX` dictionary** mapping every 64-bit `q_hash` (`semantic_id`) used in sibling `.10d`/`.m64`/`.emf`/`.qac` entries to its full UTF-8/UTF-16 RDF IRI (`UBERON:*`, `FMA:*`, `GO:*`, `DOID:*`, `HP:*`, `QUDT:*`, `intenteco`, `qualia-poiesis`) and BCP-47 labels; (b) the **6-column `NQuin` (`[s, p, o, c, m, parity]`) graph** encoding mereology (`BFO:0000050`), spatial containment (`RCC8` `ntpp`/`ec`, serous cavity $\phi_{\text{cavity}} \le -\epsilon$, radial tissue depth $d \in [0,1]$), `BranchGraph10d` physiological connectivity, `.m64` matter links, `SKL2`/`IOVR` joint limits & `#81` receipts, `L0`–`L6` stratum links, and `.vibe` creation-tree / `source-asset-licensing.n3` provenance; and (c) compiled **N3Logic / SLG-VM rules, LTL specifications, and SHACL shapes** for zero-heap `FixedArena` inference within the 42 MiB Prolog Sentinel.
2. **Level 2 — Non-Zero 64-Bit `q_hash` (`semantic_id`) Pointers on Every `.10d` Primitive (Preserving Exact `48 B` `IOVR` `#[repr(C)]` Layout):** Do **not** modify the 48-byte `#[repr(C)]` layout of `InstanceOverrideHeader` (`IOVR` `0x0302`, `archetype_ref_section.rs:166-180`). Instead, each `IOVR` (`0x0302`) slice is paired **1:1 by leaf/override index $k$** (and via `ManifoldIdentityV2` `TypedFieldRecord` ordering) with the $k$-th `ArchetypeRefRecord` (`AREF` `0x0301`, `80 B`, which already carries `taxon_or_class_iri_hash: u64`, `subresource_key_hash: u64`, and `archetype_entity_id: [u8; 16]`) or with `InstanceBatchRecord.entity_id_hash: u64` (`INST` `0x0303`, `64 B`) / `ManifoldIdentityV2.entity_id`. Every `SubmeshRange.semantic_id` (`MAT1`), `M64HoloRecord.subject_hash` (`HOLO` `0x0306`), `SdfBrickRecord` (`SDFB` `0x0307`, carrying distinct `wall_semantic_id` and `lumen_semantic_id` hashes), `Skl2Joint` (`SKL2`), `AREF`/`INST` record, `NestedStratumNodeRecord.host_anchor` & `taxon_or_ontology_iri_hash` (`MSTR` `0x0304` across `L0`–`L6`), and `AffordanceBinaryRecord.action_id` (`EONT` `17`) MUST be populated with `qualia_core_db::q_hash(canonical_iri_utf8)` indexing directly into the `.hmc`'s `.q42` vault.
3. **Level 3 — Standalone `.10d` In-Envelope `CBLD` (`0x0305`) Sync with the `.hmc`'s `.q42` Vault:** When a `.10d` is inspected standalone, its in-envelope `CBLD` (`0x0305`) section carries its local `MultilingualLexicalTerm` table and core `NQuin` slice; when packaged into an `.hmc`, all leaf `CBLD`, `MSTR`, and `EONT` graphs are losslessly merged and compiled into the `.hmc`'s authoritative `.q42` entry (with each `BundleEntry.meta` CBOR record pointing to its root `q_hash` in the `.q42` vault).
4. **Level 4 — Zero-Heap Bidirectional Spatial $\leftrightarrow$ `.q42` Inference (`#47`, `#82`, `#114`, `#118`):** GPU picking or cutaway intersection (`(instance_slot, semantic_id_u64, surface_region_2bit)`) joins in $O(1)$ against the `.hmc`'s embedded `.q42` vault for SPARQL / SLG-VM / N3Logic / RCC8 / LTL / Pearl `do()` inference (`#47`, `#114`), while `.q42` inference results return exact `semantic_id` (`u64`) sets to drive QualiaPortal/Poet highlights, cutaways (`#82`), and `IOVR`/`SKL2` actuation.
5. **Level 5 — Three-Tier Semantic Kinematic Macros, Multi-Joint Synergies & Algorithmic Motion in `semantic_model.q42` (`WP-03`, `WP-08`, `WP-17`):** Because every `SKL2` joint, `AREF`/`IOVR` leaf PGA motor (`local_pga_motor: [f32; 8]`), `MRP1` morph target, `FEMC` muscle/enthesis binding, and `EONT` `AffordanceBinaryRecord.action_id` is indexed by `q_hash` in `semantic_model.q42`, the `.q42` vault supports three composable tiers of motion without hardcoding bone indices: (a) **Tier A (Simple Semantic Joint Commands)** addressing a single joint/leaf by IRI within its `#81` `.q42` ROM/clearance envelope; (b) **Tier B (Declarative Multi-Joint Kinematic Macros & Synergies)** where high-level macro IRIs in `semantic_model.q42` (bound to `EONT` `action_id` and Poet `pulse`/`aura` `#118`, e.g., `"macro:cervical_flexion_sweep"`, `"macro:hand_precision_grasp"`, `"macro:gait_cycle"`, `"macro:peristaltic_wave"`) expand via N3Logic rules into coordinated multi-joint PGA motor trajectories (`#73`), `ABSM` 2D blend-spaces (`#23`), muscle recruitment (`#26`), and `MRP1` morph weights; and (c) **Tier C (Closed-Loop Algorithmic & Physics-Coupled Controllers)** where `#22` (DCA multibody IK), `#66` (turgor/paraheliotropic/hemodynamic flux), `#61` (aeroelastic wind), and `#128` (6-DOF vehicle/vessel dynamics) read physical parameters from `semantic_model.q42` + `.m64` and write deterministic PGA motor updates to `SKL2`/`IOVR`.

Following the updated reviewer handoff in [`C:\github\synth\docs\CODEX-SWARM-REVIEW-HANDOFF.md`](file:///c:/github/synth/docs/CODEX-SWARM-REVIEW-HANDOFF.md) (`386 / 390` wired IDs after wiring `#134` in `native_public_sdfb_attribution.rs` and `#146` in `swarm_earth/native_trophic_receipt.rs`, plus `#118` domain-separated Ed25519 + SHACL credential gate, nested `.hmc` cutaway DFS traversal, fractional DICOM-SEG, and offline QUBO) and the specialist architectural review in [`C:\github\synth\docs\CODEX-SWARM-EXPERT-MORPHISM-REPORT.md`](file:///c:/github/synth/docs/CODEX-SWARM-EXPERT-MORPHISM-REPORT.md), the remaining to-do list is structured into three sequential milestone gates:

  2. **`#195` `qualia.bio.simulate_immunology_wound_and_seir_network`** (`native_tissue_compartment_receipt.rs`, mirroring `native_trophic_receipt.rs` in `#146`): Supports `"verify_archived_tissue_and_compartment_receipt"` (standalone archived-result verifier checking closed-pool conservation within $10^{-12}$, non-negativity, volume-filling cap $0 \le \rho \le 1$, monotonic `.10d` `FEMC` collagen scar-stiffness recovery, next-generation matrix spectral radius $R_0 = \beta/\gamma$, and 6-column `NQuin` `.q42` readback) and `"step_sterile_wound_repair_fixture"` (bounded 1D dimensionless volume-filling Patlak-Keller-Segel sterile wound-repair step);
